Verstappen first experienced [[formula racing]] at [[Pembrey Circuit|Pembrey]] in August 2013 with [[MP Motorsport|Manor MP]]; he drove 160 laps across two days in a [[Barazi-Epsilon#Barazi-Epsilon FR2.0-10|Barazi-Epsilon FR2.0-10]].<ref>{{Cite web | title=Autosport 75: Why Verstappen was ready for F1 at 17 | url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/autosport-75-why-verstappen-was-ready-for-f1-at-17/10713867/ | access-date=24 May 2025 | website=www.autosport.com}}</ref><ref>{{cite web|title=Max Verstappen sensationeel in allereerste meters Formule Renault 2.0|url=http://www.racexpress.nl/formule-1/max-verstappen-sensationeel-in-allereerste-meters-formule-renault-2-0/n/53111|work=RaceXpress.nl|access-date=27 January 2014|archive-date=5 February 2015|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150205021915/http://www.racexpress.nl/formule-1/max-verstappen-sensationeel-in-allereerste-meters-formule-renault-2-0/n/53111|url-status=live}}</ref> He tested for several other [[Formula Renault 2.0]] teams that year.<ref>{{cite web|url=http://www.gpupdate.net/nl/autosport-nieuws/303024/verstappen-max-al-net-zo-snel-als-ervaren-coureurs/|title=Verstappen: "Max al net zo snel als ervaren coureurs"|website=GPUpdate.net|access-date=2 September 2017|archive-date=2 September 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170902224144/http://www.gpupdate.net/nl/autosport-nieuws/303024/verstappen-max-al-net-zo-snel-als-ervaren-coureurs/|url-status=live}}</ref> In December 2013, Verstappen tested a [[Dallara]] F311 [[Formula Three]] car run by [[Motopark Academy]].<ref>{{Cite web|title=Max Verstappen flies in Formula 3 test debut|url=https://www.autosport.com/fia-f3/news/max-verstappen-flies-in-formula-3-test-debut-4471773/4471773/|access-date=20 January 2022|website=[[Autosport]]|archive-date=20 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220120130449/https://www.autosport.com/fia-f3/news/max-verstappen-flies-in-formula-3-test-debut-4471773/4471773/|url-status=live}}</ref> An additional Formula Renault test followed in December at [[Circuito de Jerez]]. Driving for [[Josef Kaufmann Racing]], Verstappen went faster than Formula Renault regulars like [[Steijn Schothorst]] and [[Matt Parry]].<ref>{{cite web|title=Max op haar na snelste bij laatste FR 2.0 test van 2013|url=http://www.verstappen.nl/?locatie=bericht&id=8323|work=Verstappen Info Page|access-date=27 January 2014|archive-date=5 February 2015|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150205012246/http://www.verstappen.nl/?locatie=bericht&id=8323|url-status=live}}</ref> At the [[Circuit Ricardo Tormo]] near [[Valencia]], Verstappen set a faster lap time than the more experienced driver [[Eddie Cheever III]].<ref>{{cite web|title=Max fenomenaal snel bij zijn eerste F3 test op Valencia|url=http://www.verstappen.nl/?locatie=bericht&id=8326|work=Verstappen Info Page|access-date=27 January 2014|archive-date=5 February 2015|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150205012216/http://www.verstappen.nl/?locatie=bericht&id=8326|url-status=live}}</ref>

==== 2014: Florida Winter Series and Formula Three ====
On 16 January 2014, it was announced Verstappen would make his [[junior formulae]] debut in the non-championship [[Florida Winter Series]].<ref>{{cite web|title=Max maakt autosportdebuut in Florida Winter Series|url=http://www.verstappen.nl/?locatie=bericht&id=8336|work=Verstappen Info Page|access-date=27 January 2014|archive-date=5 February 2015|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150205012639/http://www.verstappen.nl/?locatie=bericht&id=8336|url-status=live}}</ref> On 5 February, at the second race weekend, Verstappen won the race at [[Palm Beach International Raceway]] after he started from pole.<ref>{{cite web|title=Verstappen wint eerste autorace na regen en rode vlag|url=http://www.gpupdate.net/nl/autosport-nieuws/306301/verstappen-wint-eerste-autorace-na-rode-vlag|access-date=16 May 2016|archive-date=6 March 2016|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160306004252/http://www.gpupdate.net/nl/autosport-nieuws/306301/verstappen-wint-eerste-autorace-na-rode-vlag/|url-status=live}}</ref> A few weeks later, Verstappen won his second race of the series at [[Homestead–Miami Speedway]] after beating [[Nicholas Latifi]] by 0.004 seconds.<ref>{{Citation|title=Max Verstappen Onboard FWS, Homestead race 3, 19/02/2014 – Max beats Latifi by 0.004s!| date=11 March 2014 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=rJQHa6nHV04|access-date=20 January 2022|archive-date=13 October 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221013203425/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=rJQHa6nHV04|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|title=Formule 1 nieuws – Lees het laatste nieuws over Formule 1|url=https://nl.motorsport.com/f1/news/|access-date=20 January 2022|website=nl.motorsport.com|language=nl|archive-date=18 March 2020|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200318043055/https://www.autosport.com/|url-status=live}}</ref>

Following his single-seater debut in the inaugural Florida Winter Series, Verstappen drove in the [[2014 FIA Formula 3 European Championship]] for [[Van Amersfoort Racing]].<ref>{{cite web |url=http://www.fiaf3europe.com/en/news/Max-Verstappen-new-Red-Bull-Junior.html |title=Max Verstappen new part of the Red Bull Junior Team |publisher=Fédération Internationale de l'Automobile |access-date=20 August 2014 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150924012244/http://www.fiaf3europe.com/en/news/Max-Verstappen-new-Red-Bull-Junior.html |archive-date=24 September 2015}}</ref> Aged sixteen, Verstappen achieved ten victories in total—including a record six consecutive wins at [[Spa-Francorchamps]] and the [[Norisring]]—along with eight retirements and one missed start, becoming the youngest race winner and polesitter in [[Formula Three]] history at the [[Hockenheimring]]; he placed third in the overall standings, with the most wins.<ref>{{cite web|title=Max Verstappen|url=https://www.redbull.com/gb-en/athlete/max-verstappen-bio|access-date=29 January 2022|publisher=Red Bull |archive-date=29 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220129030707/https://www.redbull.com/gb-en/athlete/max-verstappen-bio|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|title=2014 European F3 Championship|url=https://www.motorsportmagazine.com/database/championships/2014-european-f3-championship/|access-date=19 January 2022|work=Motor Sport Magazine|archive-date=22 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220122204809/https://www.motorsportmagazine.com/database/championships/2014-european-f3-championship/|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|title=Max Verstappen: what I learned in F3|date=2 August 2016 |url=https://www.redbull.com/gb-en/max-verstappen-interview-european-f3-review-2014|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=Red Bull|archive-date=22 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220122204809/https://www.redbull.com/gb-en/max-verstappen-interview-european-f3-review-2014|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.autosport.com/fia-f3/news/hockenheim-european-f3-max-verstappen-takes-maiden-f3-win-4471969/4471969/|title=Hockenheim European F3: Max Verstappen takes maiden F3 win|date=4 May 2014|access-date=28 November 2024|website=[[Autosport]]|first=Marcus|last=Simmons|archive-date=3 December 2024|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20241203010943/https://www.autosport.com/fia-f3/news/hockenheim-european-f3-max-verstappen-takes-maiden-f3-win-4471969/4471969/|url-status=live}}</ref>

== Formula One career ==
During the first practice session at the [[2014 Japanese Grand Prix]], Verstappen replaced [[Jean-Éric Vergne]] as part of his preparation for a full-time seat at [[Scuderia Toro Rosso|Toro Rosso]] in the {{f1|2015}} season.<ref>{{Cite web|last=Benson|first=Andrew|date=29 September 2014|title=Max Verstappen: Teenager to drive in practice at Japanese Grand Prix|url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/formula1/29408212|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160601084502/http://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/formula1/29408212|archive-date=1 June 2016|access-date=23 July 2016|publisher=[[BBC Sport]]}}</ref> Aged 17 years and three days, Verstappen was the youngest person in history to participate in a Formula One race weekend.<ref>{{Cite web |date=24 July 2016 |title=Japanese Grand Prix 2014: History-maker Max Verstappen flies like the wind as typhoon zooms closer |url=http://www.independent.co.uk/sport/motor-racing/japanese-grand-prix-2014-history-maker-max-verstappen-flies-like-the-wind-as-typhoon-zooms-closer-9773924.html |access-date=22 January 2022 |website=[[The Independent]]|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160724110219/http://www.independent.co.uk/sport/motor-racing/japanese-grand-prix-2014-history-maker-max-verstappen-flies-like-the-wind-as-typhoon-zooms-closer-9773924.html |archive-date=24 July 2016 |url-status=dead}}</ref> In August 2014, Verstappen joined the [[Red Bull Junior Team]] after testing a Formula Renault 3.5 car. He also considered an offer from [[Mercedes-Benz in Formula One|Mercedes]] to join their [[Mercedes Junior Team|driver development programme]].<ref>{{cite news|url=http://www.nu.nl/sport/3850611/max-verstappen-per-direct-opgenomen-in-opleiding-red-bull.html|title=Max Verstappen per direct opgenomen in opleiding Red Bull|date=12 August 2014|work=verstappen.nl |access-date=18 August 2014|publisher=Nu.nl|archive-date=22 May 2021|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210522154400/https://www.nu.nl/sport/3850611/max-verstappen-per-direct-opgenomen-in-opleiding-red-bull.html|url-status=live}}</ref>

===Toro Rosso (2015–2016) ===
[[File:Max Verstappen 2015 Malaysia Q2.jpg|thumb|left|Verstappen (pictured at the [[2015 Malaysian Grand Prix]]) became the youngest driver in Formula One history at the {{F1GP|2015|Australian}}, aged 17.]]
Verstappen then became the [[List of Formula One driver records#Youngest drivers to start a race|youngest driver to start a World Championship race]] through joining Toro Rosso's race drivers' line-up with [[Carlos Sainz Jr.|Carlos Sainz]] as his teammate, in his Grand Prix debut as a full-time driver at the [[2015 Australian Grand Prix]] at the age of {{Age in years and days|1997|9|30|2015|3|15}}{{Emdash}}breaking [[Jaime Alguersuari]]'s existing record by almost two years.<ref>{{cite news|title=Max Verstappen to race with Toro Rosso F1 team in 2015|website=Autosport|url=http://www.autosport.com/news/report.php/id/115435|date=18 August 2014|access-date=18 August 2014|archive-date=1 July 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170701130917/http://www.autosport.com/news/report.php/id/115435|url-status=live}}</ref> In this first race, Verstappen ran in points-scoring positions until he was forced to retire due to an engine failure.<ref>{{cite news|url=http://www.autosport.com/news/report.php/id/118069|title=Lewis Hamilton beats Nico Rosberg to win|first=Lawrence|last=Barretto|website=Autosport|date=15 March 2015|access-date=29 March 2015|archive-date=16 March 2015|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150316231343/http://www.autosport.com/news/report.php/id/118069|url-status=live}}</ref> However, at the subsequent race in [[2015 Malaysian Grand Prix|Malaysia]], Verstappen qualified sixth and finished the race in seventh place, scoring his first Formula One points aged {{Age in years and days|1997|9|30|2015|3|29}}, breaking the record of the then-[[List of Formula One driver records#Youngest drivers to score points|youngest driver to score World Championship points]].<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.telegraph.co.uk/sport/motorsport/formulaone/sebastian-vettel/11502398/Malaysian-GP-2015-Sebastian-Vettel-picks-up-surprise-win-for-Ferrari-to-blow-world-title-wide-open.html |archive-url=https://ghostarchive.org/archive/20220110/https://www.telegraph.co.uk/sport/motorsport/formulaone/sebastian-vettel/11502398/Malaysian-GP-2015-Sebastian-Vettel-picks-up-surprise-win-for-Ferrari-to-blow-world-title-wide-open.html |archive-date=10 January 2022 |url-access=subscription |url-status=live|title=Sebastian Vettel picks up surprise win for Ferrari to blow world title wide open|first=Daniel|last=Johnson|work=[[The Daily Telegraph]]|date=29 March 2015|access-date=29 March 2015|quote=Max Verstappen took seventh to become the youngest points scorer in F1 history, at 17 years and 180 days.}}{{cbignore}}</ref>

At the [[2015 Monaco Grand Prix]], Verstappen was involved in a high-speed collision with [[Romain Grosjean]], after clipping the back of Grosjean's [[Lotus F1|Lotus]] on the approach to the tight first corner, Sainte Devote, and crashed into the barriers at high speed.<ref>{{Citation|title=5 Crazy Onboards {{!}} Monaco Grand Prix| date=22 May 2018 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=sMde0gU-KhQ|access-date=20 January 2022|archive-date=20 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220120132014/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=sMde0gU-KhQ|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|title=Monaco GP: Max Verstappen penalised for crash with Romain Grosjean|url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/monaco-gp-max-verstappen-penalised-for-crash-with-romain-grosjean-5006608/5006608/|access-date=20 January 2022|website=Autosport|date=24 May 2015 |archive-date=17 December 2021|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20211217052426/https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/monaco-gp-max-verstappen-penalised-for-crash-with-romain-grosjean-5006608/5006608/|url-status=live}}</ref> Verstappen was given a five-place grid penalty for causing the accident, and was branded "dangerous" by [[Williams Grand Prix Engineering|Williams]] driver [[Felipe Massa]].<ref>William Esler, "[http://www.skysports.com/f1/news/31381/9875227/max-verstappen-hits-back-at-felipe-massa-of-monaco-crash-criticism Max Verstappen hits back at Felipe Massa over Monaco crash criticism] {{webarchive |url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160611182531/http://www.skysports.com/f1/news/31381/9875227/max-verstappen-hits-back-at-felipe-massa-of-monaco-crash-criticism |date=11 June 2016}}", [[Sky Sports]], 2015.</ref>

Verstappen continued to regularly finish in the points as well as achieving his best finish of the 2015 season in [[2015 Hungarian Grand Prix|Hungary]] by finishing fourth,<ref>{{Cite web|title=9 things we learned from the Hungarian Grand Prix|date=26 July 2015 |url=https://www.redbull.com/gb-en/2015-hungarian-grand-prix-f1-report|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=Red Bull|archive-date=10 March 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220310191111/https://www.redbull.com/gb-en/2015-hungarian-grand-prix-f1-report|url-status=live}}</ref> and equalled this result at the [[2015 United States Grand Prix|United States Grand Prix]].<ref>{{Cite web|title=Hamilton seals 2015 title with thrilling Austin win|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/headlines/2015/10/hamilton-seals-2015-title-with-thrilling-austin-win.html|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=Formula One|archive-date=25 December 2021|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20211225175054/https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/headlines/2015/10/hamilton-seals-2015-title-with-thrilling-austin-win.html|url-status=live}}</ref> At the end of the season, Verstappen received three awards at the [[FIA Prize Giving Ceremony]], for "Rookie of the Year", "Personality of the Year" and "Action of the Year", for his overtake on [[Felipe Nasr]] on the outside of the Blanchimont corner at the [[2015 Belgian Grand Prix|Belgian Grand Prix]].<ref>{{cite web|last1=Galloway|first1=James|title=Lewis Hamilton & Mercedes officially crowned 2015 world champions|url=http://www.skysports.com/f1/news/24181/10087594/lewis-hamilton-mercedes-officially-crowned-2015-world-champions|publisher=Sky Sports|access-date=5 December 2015|date=5 December 2015|archive-date=5 December 2015|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20151205171818/http://www.skysports.com/f1/news/24181/10087594/lewis-hamilton-mercedes-officially-crowned-2015-world-champions|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Citation|title=Top 10 Moments of Max Verstappen Magic in F1| date=8 January 2020 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=B4pF4bMwYYI|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=19 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220119223659/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=B4pF4bMwYYI|url-status=live}}</ref>

[[File:Verstappen Bahrain 2016.jpg|alt=|thumb|Verstappen at the [[2016 Bahrain Grand Prix]]]]
Verstappen began the 2016 season at Toro Rosso, again alongside Sainz.<ref>{{Cite web|title=Max Verstappen and Carlos Sainz to continue for Toro Rosso in 2016|url=https://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12471/10084883/toro-rosso-have-confirmed-that-max-verstappen-and-carlos-sainz-will-race-in-2016|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=Sky Sports|archive-date=19 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220119221049/https://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12471/10084883/toro-rosso-have-confirmed-that-max-verstappen-and-carlos-sainz-will-race-in-2016|url-status=live}}</ref> Verstappen qualified fifth for the opening race of the season in [[2016 Australian Grand Prix|Australia]], but during the race made several radio calls to his team due to frustration at being behind Sainz on track before Verstappen's Toro Rosso came into contact with his teammate's car whilst attempting to pass him with three laps to go, and he eventually finished tenth.<ref>"[https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/formula1/35854819 Max Verstappen: Australian GP shows moody side of Toro Rosso teenager] {{webarchive |url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160323010100/http://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/formula1/35854819 |date=23 March 2016}}", BBC Sport, 2016.</ref> Verstappen enjoyed a more successful weekend at the following race in [[2016 Bahrain Grand Prix|Bahrain]], finishing sixth to score Toro Rosso's first-ever points at the Sakhir circuit.<ref>"[http://www.gpupdate.net/en/f1-news/337624/verstappen-scores-str-s-first-bahrain-points/ Verstappen scores STR's first Bahrain points] {{webarchive |url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160511232826/http://www.gpupdate.net/en/f1-news/337624/verstappen-scores-str-s-first-bahrain-points/ |date=11 May 2016}}", ''GP Update'', 2016.</ref>

===Red Bull (2016–present)===
====2016: Youngest Grand Prix winner====
[[File:Verstappen Spain 2016.jpg|thumb|left|Verstappen celebrating victory at the [[2016 Spanish Grand Prix]], in his first race for the Red Bull Racing team]]

On 5 May 2016, following the {{F1GP|2016|Russian}}, Red Bull Racing announced that Verstappen would be replacing Daniil Kvyat from the [[2016 Spanish Grand Prix|Spanish Grand Prix]] onwards, with Kvyat returning to Toro Rosso.<ref>{{Cite web|title=Red Bull replace Kvyat with Verstappen|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/headlines/2016/5/red-bull-replace-kvyat-with-verstappen.html|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=Formula One|archive-date=20 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220120221159/https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/headlines/2016/5/red-bull-replace-kvyat-with-verstappen.html|url-status=live}}</ref> According to Red Bull Team Principal [[Christian Horner]], "Max has proven to be an outstanding young talent. His performance at Toro Rosso has been impressive so far and we are pleased to give him the opportunity to drive for Red Bull Racing."<ref name="RBR Swap">{{cite news|url=http://www.redbullracing.com/article/new-line-spain|title=New line-up for Spain|work=RedBullRacing.com|date=5 May 2016|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160508080016/http://www.redbullracing.com/article/new-line-spain|archive-date=8 May 2016}}</ref> After qualifying fourth for the Spanish Grand Prix,<ref name="Verstappen 4th in Qualifying">{{cite news|url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/live/formula1/35946953|title=Hamilton takes pole in Spain|publisher=BBC Sport|date=14 May 2016|access-date=12 February 2018|archive-date=20 December 2018|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20181220042109/https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/live/formula1/35946953|url-status=live}}</ref> Verstappen rose to second behind teammate [[Daniel Ricciardo]] on the opening lap after Mercedes teammates [[Lewis Hamilton]] and [[Nico Rosberg]] crashed out of the race.<ref>{{Cite web|title=WATCH: Mercedes collision puts Hamilton and Rosberg out on lap 1|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/headlines/2016/5/mercedes-collision-puts-hamilton-and-rosberg-out-on-lap-one.html|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=Formula One|archive-date=24 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220124222604/https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/headlines/2016/5/mercedes-collision-puts-hamilton-and-rosberg-out-on-lap-one.html|url-status=live}}</ref> Verstappen took the race lead as he was placed on a two-stop rather than the same three-stop strategy as Ricciardo, and he held off [[Scuderia Ferrari|Ferrari]]'s [[Kimi Räikkönen]] in the later stages of the race to take his first Formula One victory.<ref>{{Citation|title=2016 Spanish Grand Prix: Race Highlights| date=7 May 2017 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3B3-C914d_c|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=21 April 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220421042514/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3B3-C914d_c|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Citation|title=Verstappen's Maiden Win {{!}} Spanish Grand Prix 2016| date=28 January 2017 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hohuswdeznA|access-date=21 January 2022|archive-date=13 October 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221013203403/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hohuswdeznA|url-status=live}}</ref> By doing so he displaced [[Sebastian Vettel]] as the youngest driver ever to win a Formula One Grand Prix at the age of 18 years and 228 days.<ref name=":0">{{Cite web|url=https://www.theguardian.com/sport/live/2016/may/15/f1-spanish-gp-live-lewis-hamilton-nico-rosberg|title=F1: Max Verstappen wins Spanish GP after Lewis Hamilton and Nico Rosberg crash – live!|last=Ostlere|first=Lawrence|date=15 May 2016|website=The Guardian|access-date=15 May 2016|archive-date=15 May 2016|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160515143832/https://www.theguardian.com/sport/live/2016/may/15/f1-spanish-gp-live-lewis-hamilton-nico-rosberg|url-status=live}}</ref>

In his first eight races with Red Bull, he achieved six top-five finishes, including four podiums.<ref name="Standings">{{cite web|url=https://www.statsf1.com/en/max-verstappen/grand-prix.aspx|title=Max Verstappen – Grands Prix|access-date=13 June 2022|website=StatsF1|archive-date=13 June 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220613195714/https://www.statsf1.com/en/max-verstappen/grand-prix.aspx|url-status=live}}</ref>

During the [[2016 Belgian Grand Prix|Belgian Grand Prix]], Verstappen collided with Räikkönen at the first corner, pushed Vettel, Räikkönen and Pérez wide at ''Les Combes'', and aggressively blocked Räikkönen on the Kemmel straight.<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/formula1/37226080|title=Formula 1: Max Verstappen gets under the skin of top drivers|publisher=BBC Sport|date=31 August 2016|access-date=4 November 2016|archive-date=13 November 2016|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20161113170546/http://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/formula1/37226080|url-status=live}}</ref> Verstappen was criticised for his driving, with Räikkönen saying that he was "going to cause a huge accident sooner or later." Christian Horner noted that the driving was "on the edge", and that Verstappen will "look at it and learn for future races."<ref>{{Cite web |last=Saunders |first=Nate  |date=29 August 2016 |title=Horner: Verstappen will learn from Belgium |url=http://www.espn.co.uk/f1/story/_/id/17413337/christian-horner-says-max-verstappen-learn-belgium |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20161107083945/http://www.espn.co.uk/f1/story/_/id/17413337/christian-horner-says-max-verstappen-learn-belgium |archive-date=7 November 2016 |access-date=4 November 2016 |publisher=ESPN}}</ref> In September, Formula One director [[Charlie Whiting]] called in Verstappen for a discussion, and later gave him a 'gentle warning' due to his aggressive driving.<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/formula1/37258313|title=Italian GP: Max Verstappen given 'gentle warning' by FIA|publisher=BBC Sport|date=2 September 2016|access-date=4 November 2016|archive-date=6 September 2016|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160906224118/http://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/formula1/37258313|url-status=live}}</ref> However, in October, drivers' concerns about Verstappen's defensive tactics led the FIA to disallow moving under braking.<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/37741994|title=Formula 1 outlaws moving under braking after protests over Max Verstappen|publisher=BBC Sport|date=22 October 2016|access-date=12 February 2018|archive-date=2 September 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170902235036/http://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/37741994|url-status=live}}</ref>

At the [[2016 Brazilian Grand Prix]], Verstappen qualified fourth.<ref>{{cite web|title=Formula 1 Grande Premio do Brasil 2016 – Qualifying |url=http://www.formula1.com/en/results.html/2016/races/956/brazil/qualifying.html |publisher=Formula One |access-date=17 November 2016 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20161117112850/http://www.formula1.com/en/results.html/2016/races/956/brazil/qualifying.html |archive-date=17 November 2016 |url-status=dead}}</ref> In a rain affected race, he almost hit the barrier after he slid on the main straight due to a loss of traction, causing [[oversteer]].<ref>{{Citation|title=Verstappen's Amazing Save in Brazil {{!}} F1 is...Heart-in-Mouth| date=14 March 2017 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wQCeQKQ7_vI|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=10 March 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220310040858/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wQCeQKQ7_vI|url-status=live}}</ref> After an additional tyre change from intermediates back to rain tyres, he ran in 16th place with just 15 laps remaining. Verstappen then made several overtakes in quick succession during the closing laps to eventually finish on the podium in third place.<ref>{{cite web|last1=Galloway |first1=James |title=Max Verstappen stole the show in Brazil GP, says Mercedes' Toto Wolff |url=http://www.skysports.com/f1/news/31381/10657138/max-verstappen-stole-the-show-in-brazil-gp-says-mercedes-toto-wolff |publisher=Sky Sports |access-date=17 November 2016 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20161117112959/http://www.skysports.com/f1/news/31381/10657138/max-verstappen-stole-the-show-in-brazil-gp-says-mercedes-toto-wolff |archive-date=17 November 2016 |date=14 November 2016 |url-status=dead}}</ref> He received considerable praise for his performance: rival team Mercedes' team principal, [[Toto Wolff]], labelled it "The Verstappen Show", and described Verstappen's drive as "physics..being redefined".<ref>{{cite web|last1=Gill |first1=Pete |last2=Esler |first2=William |title=Max Verstappen's Brazilian GP drive hailed as 'one of the best ever in F1' |url=http://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12040/10656712/max-verstappens-brazilian-gp-drive-hailed-as-one-of-the-best-ever-in-f1 |publisher=Sky Sports |access-date=17 November 2016 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20161117113003/http://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12040/10656712/max-verstappens-brazilian-gp-drive-hailed-as-one-of-the-best-ever-in-f1 |archive-date=17 November 2016 |date=14 November 2016 |url-status=dead}}</ref><ref>{{Citation|title=Max Verstappen's Masterclass {{!}} 2016 Brazilian Grand Prix| date=7 November 2017 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JO3q69x2sXA|access-date=22 January 2022|archive-date=22 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220122133251/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JO3q69x2sXA|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|title=Verstappen's Brazil drive earns Senna/Schumacher comparisons|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/headlines/2016/11/f1-verstappen-brazil-gp-senna-schumacher-comparisons.html|access-date=22 January 2022|publisher=Formula One|archive-date=22 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220122133250/https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/headlines/2016/11/f1-verstappen-brazil-gp-senna-schumacher-comparisons.html|url-status=live}}</ref> However, Verstappen came under criticism from four-time world champion Sebastian Vettel, who stated that Verstappen had pushed him off the track at the Junção corner late in the race. The race stewards did not share Vettel's view and decided that no reprimand was warranted.<ref>{{cite web|last1=Edmondson |first1=Laurence |title=Vettel: Verstappen move was 'not correct' |url=http://www.espn.co.uk/f1/story/_/id/18041493/max-verstappen-move-was-not-correct |publisher=ESPN |access-date=17 November 2016 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20161117113518/http://www.espn.co.uk/f1/story/_/id/18041493/max-verstappen-move-was-not-correct |archive-date=17 November 2016 |date=14 November 2016 |url-status=dead}}</ref>

====2017–2018: Reliability issues and rise to prominence====
[[File:Max Verstappen overtaking Lewis Hamilton 2017 Malaysia 2.jpg|thumb|right|Verstappen took his second career victory at the {{F1GP|2017|Malaysian}} in {{F1|2017}}.|alt=Verstappen overtaking Lewis Hamilton for the lead of the 2017 Malaysian Grand Prix]]
During the first 14 races of the 2017 season, Verstappen suffered seven retirements, four due to mechanical issues,<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/41068214|title=Max Verstappen: Red Bull's reliability unacceptable|publisher=BBC Sport|date=27 August 2017|access-date=12 February 2018|archive-date=30 August 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170830010414/http://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/41068214|url-status=live}}</ref> and three due to first lap collisions in [[2017 Spanish Grand Prix|Spain]], [[2017 Austrian Grand Prix|Austria]] and [[2017 Singapore Grand Prix|Singapore]].<ref>[http://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12433/10877127/spanish-gp-valtteri-bottas-triggers-max-verstappen-kimi-raikkonen-crash Spanish GP: Valtteri Bottas triggers Max Verstappen-Kimi Raikkonen crash] {{webarchive |url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170628083609/http://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12433/10877127/spanish-gp-valtteri-bottas-triggers-max-verstappen-kimi-raikkonen-crash |date=28 June 2017}} – James Galloway and Emily McNally, Sky Sports, 14 May 2017</ref> Of the races he finished, however, he claimed a third place in [[2017 Chinese Grand Prix|China]], and in another five races he finished fourth or fifth.<ref name="Standings"/>

From the [[2017 Malaysian Grand Prix|Malaysian Grand Prix]] onward, Verstappen enjoyed a surge of success. He won his second Formula One race at the 2017 Malaysian Grand Prix, a day after his 20th birthday, passing then three-time champion Lewis Hamilton for the lead in the early stages of the race.<ref>{{cite web|date=1 October 2017|title=Max Verstappen beats Lewis Hamilton to win F1's Malaysian Grand Prix|url=http://www.theguardian.com/sport/2017/oct/01/max-verstappen-lewis-hamilton-f1-malaysian-grand-prix-race-report-formula-one|access-date=19 January 2022|website=The Guardian|archive-date=15 December 2021|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20211215041014/https://www.theguardian.com/sport/2017/oct/01/max-verstappen-lewis-hamilton-f1-malaysian-grand-prix-race-report-formula-one|url-status=live}}</ref> He finished second in the following [[2017 Japanese Grand Prix|race in Japan]].<ref>{{cite web|title=2017 Formula 1 Japanese Grand Prix – Race Result|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/results.html/2017/races/974/japan/race-result.html|publisher=Formula One|access-date=24 October 2017|archive-date=7 October 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20171007170355/https://www.formula1.com/en/results.html/2017/races/974/japan/race-result.html|url-status=live}}</ref> He then finished third at the [[2017 United States Grand Prix|United States]] Grand Prix, but was classified fourth after his final lap overtake on Kimi Räikkönen was deemed illegal.<ref>{{cite web|title=2017 Formula 1 United States Grand Prix – Race Result|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/results.html/2017/races/975/united-states.html|publisher=Formula One|access-date=24 October 2017|archive-date=22 October 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20171022215609/https://www.formula1.com/en/results.html/2017/races/975/united-states.html|url-status=live}}</ref> He won his third Formula One race at the [[2017 Mexican Grand Prix|Mexican Grand Prix]], after passing [[Sebastian Vettel]] on the opening lap.<ref>{{Cite web|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/results.html/2017/races/976/mexico.html|title=Formula 1 Gran Premio de México 2017 – Race Result|publisher=Formula One|access-date=29 October 2017|archive-date=27 October 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20171027235005/https://www.formula1.com/en/results.html/2017/races/976/mexico.html|url-status=live}}</ref>

[[File:FIA F1 Austria 2018 Nr. 33 Verstappen.jpg|thumb|left|Verstappen at the [[2018 Austrian Grand Prix]]]]
In the first six races of the {{F1|2018}} season, Verstappen had been involved in at least one incident in each race. In [[2018 Australian Grand Prix|Australia]], he qualified fourth but fell behind [[Kevin Magnussen]] at the start.<ref>{{Cite web|title=F1 Results – 2018 Australian Grand Prix|date=24 March 2018 |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/2018/australian-grand-prix/results/qualifying|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=BBC Sport|archive-date=19 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220119232004/https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/2018/australian-grand-prix/results/qualifying|url-status=live}}</ref> In his attempts to retake the position he ran wide multiple times and damaged his car, with a spin causing him to fall further down the order. He recovered to eventually finish the race in sixth place.<ref>{{Cite news|url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/135028/early-damage-a-factor-in-verstappen-spin|title=Early damage a factor in Max Verstappen's Australian GP spin|last1=Mitchell|first1=Scott|date=25 March 2018|website=Autosport|publisher=[[Motorsport Network]]|last2=Cozens|first2=Jack|access-date=30 May 2018|archive-date=2 July 2020|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200702202827/https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/135028/early-damage-a-factor-in-verstappen-spin|url-status=live}}</ref> At the next race in [[2018 Bahrain Grand Prix|Bahrain]], he crashed during qualifying and started in 15th place.<ref>{{Cite news|url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/red-bull-admits-foot-oscillation-caused-verstappen-bahrain-crash-5322006/5322006/|last1=Cooper|first1=Adam|title=Red Bull admits 'foot oscillation' caused Verstappen Bahrain crash|date=13 April 2018|website=Autosport|access-date=30 May 2018|archive-date=2 July 2020|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200702202828/https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/red-bull-admits-foot-oscillation-caused-verstappen-bahrain-crash-5322006/5322006/|url-status=live}}</ref> He had a productive first lap after which he found himself in the points while challenging Lewis Hamilton. He attempted an overtake on the reigning World Champion at the start of lap two, but collided with the Mercedes driver and suffered a puncture that ultimately led to suspension damage, forcing him out of the race.<ref>{{Cite news|url=http://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12514/11316602/bahrain-gp-sebastian-vettel-holds-off-valtteri-bottas-for-victory|title=Bahrain GP: Sebastian Vettel holds off Valtteri Bottas for victory|last=Gill|first=Pete|date=9 April 2018|publisher=[[Sky Sports F1]]|access-date=30 May 2018|archive-date=13 June 2018|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180613092534/http://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12514/11316602/bahrain-gp-sebastian-vettel-holds-off-valtteri-bottas-for-victory|url-status=live}}</ref>

At the next race in [[2018 Chinese Grand Prix|China]], Verstappen qualified fifth and had moved up to third at the end of the first lap.<ref>{{Citation|title=2018 Chinese Grand Prix: Race Highlights| date=15 April 2018 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=uiub1DnUnkg|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=19 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220119232004/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=uiub1DnUnkg|url-status=live}}</ref> Both Red Bull drivers pitted for fresh tyres during a safety car which left them with a tyre advantage over the front-runners ahead. In an overtake attempt on [[Sebastian Vettel]] for third place, Verstappen collided with the championship leader, causing him to fall to eighth and receive a 10-second penalty.<ref>{{Citation|title=Verstappen Runs into Vettel {{!}} 2018 Chinese Grand Prix| date=15 April 2018 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GR3Ls5tcp5A|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=13 October 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221013203419/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GR3Ls5tcp5A|url-status=live}}</ref> He recovered to fourth place, with his penalty causing him to be classified fifth. Teammate Ricciardo went on to win the race.<ref>{{Cite news|url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/135405/ricciardo-wins-vettel-and-verstappen-collide|title=Chinese Grand Prix: Ricciardo wins, Verstappen and Vettel collide|last=Mitchell|first=Scott|date=15 April 2018|website=Autosport|publisher=Motorsport Network|access-date=30 May 2018|archive-date=16 April 2018|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180416043910/https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/135405/ricciardo-wins-vettel-and-verstappen-collide|url-status=live}}</ref> In [[2018 Azerbaijan Grand Prix|Azerbaijan]], Verstappen was embroiled in a race-long battle with Ricciardo for fourth place. After numerous position changes between the two teammates during the race, Ricciardo ran into the back of Verstappen during an overtake attempt from which the Dutchman aggressively defended, causing the retirement of both cars.<ref>{{citation|title=All The Angles: Ricciardo and Verstappen Crash {{!}} 2018 Azerbaijan Grand Prix| date=29 April 2018 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=36YFQTFzZwU|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=13 October 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221013203426/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=36YFQTFzZwU|url-status=live}}</ref> Both drivers were blamed by the team and reprimanded by the stewards.<ref>{{Cite news|url=http://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12433/11352821/max-verstappen-daniel-ricciardo-reprimanded-for-all-red-bull-azerbaijan-gp-crash|title=Max Verstappen, Daniel Ricciardo reprimanded for all-Red Bull Azerbaijan GP crash|last=Galloway|first=James|date=30 April 2018|publisher=Sky Sports F1|access-date=30 May 2018|archive-date=11 June 2018|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180611131807/http://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12433/11352821/max-verstappen-daniel-ricciardo-reprimanded-for-all-red-bull-azerbaijan-gp-crash|url-status=live}}</ref> Verstappen bounced back in [[2018 Spanish Grand Prix|Spain]] with his first podium of the season by finishing third behind the Mercedes drivers, holding off [[Sebastian Vettel]]. However, the race was also not without incident as he had run into the back of [[Lance Stroll]] during the virtual safety car period, causing minor front wing damage.<ref>{{Cite news|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/features/2018/5/winners-and-losers--spanish-grand-prix-edition.html|title=Winners and Losers – Spanish Grand Prix edition|date=14 May 2018|publisher=Formula One|access-date=30 May 2018|archive-date=14 May 2018|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180514153208/https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/features/2018/5/winners-and-losers--spanish-grand-prix-edition.html|url-status=live}}</ref>

In [[2018 Monaco Grand Prix|Monaco]], Verstappen made another error and crashed near the end of the third free practice session in an incident which closely resembled a crash he had at the same spot [[2016 Monaco Grand Prix#Qualifying|two years earlier]].<ref>{{Cite news|url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/136314/ricciardo-fastest-as-verstappen-crashes-in-fp3|title=Monaco GP F1 practice: Ricciardo fastest as Verstappen crashes in FP3|last=Mitchell|first=Scott|date=26 May 2018|website=Autosport|publisher=Motorsport Network|access-date=30 May 2018|archive-date=16 October 2018|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20181016081329/https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/136314/ricciardo-fastest-as-verstappen-crashes-in-fp3|url-status=live}}</ref> His team could not repair his car in time for qualifying and Verstappen had to start the race from the back of the grid.<ref name=":1">{{Cite news|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/44267114|title=Monaco Grand Prix: Max Verstappen crash 'unnecessary', says Red Bull's Helmut Marko|last=Benson|first=Andrew|date=26 May 2018|publisher=BBC Sport|access-date=30 May 2018|archive-date=14 June 2018|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180614165357/https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/44267114|url-status=live}}</ref> Verstappen did not compete and did not set a lap time in qualifying, therefore teammate Ricciardo pressed home Red Bull's advantage at the track by taking [[pole position]] and the race win. Verstappen managed to salvage two points by finishing ninth place, overtaking six cars on track.<ref>{{Cite news|url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/136339/ricciardo-wins-monaco-gp-despite-car-problem|title=Monaco Grand Prix: Daniel Ricciardo wins despite car problem|last=Mitchell|first=Scott|date=27 May 2018|website=Autosport|publisher=Motorsport Network|access-date=30 May 2018|archive-date=16 October 2018|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20181016032724/https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/136339/ricciardo-wins-monaco-gp-despite-car-problem|url-status=live}}</ref> Team principal Christian Horner commented on Verstappen's start of the season, saying he "needed to stop making these mistakes" and that he could "learn from his teammate", while [[Helmut Marko]], head of driver development at Red Bull, said that Verstappen was "too impatient".<ref name=":1" /> Verstappen now lay in sixth place in the championship with 35 points, only three points ahead of [[Fernando Alonso]] in the McLaren, and 37 points behind his teammate in third, who had taken two wins in the first six races.<ref>{{Cite news|last=Woolcock|first=Adam|date=15 April 2018|title=F1 2018: Daniel Ricciardo wins Chinese Grand Prix – as it happened|work=The Guardian|url=https://www.theguardian.com/sport/live/2018/apr/15/formula-one-2018-chinese-grand-prix-live|access-date=19 January 2022|issn=0261-3077|archive-date=22 April 2018|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180422223322/https://www.theguardian.com/sport/live/2018/apr/15/formula-one-2018-chinese-grand-prix-live|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|title=F1 REWIND: Watch Daniel Ricciardo's brilliant 2018 Monaco Grand Prix win|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.watch-live-join-our-stream-of-ricciardos-brilliant-2018-monaco-grand-prix.13UtM2BlgsaIn5QzBe6B4l.html|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=Formula One|archive-date=10 March 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220310074110/https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.watch-live-join-our-stream-of-ricciardos-brilliant-2018-monaco-grand-prix.13UtM2BlgsaIn5QzBe6B4l.html|url-status=live}}</ref>

In [[2018 Canadian Grand Prix|Canada]], Verstappen topped all three practice sessions and qualified third, two-tenths off Sebastian Vettel in pole position.<ref>{{Cite web|title=F1 Results – 2018 Canadian Grand Prix|date=9 June 2018 |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/2018/canadian-grand-prix/results/qualifying|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=BBC Sport|archive-date=19 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220119232005/https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/2018/canadian-grand-prix/results/qualifying|url-status=live}}</ref> He eventually finished third and set the fastest lap of the race.<ref>{{Cite web|date=10 June 2018|title=Sebastian Vettel wins Canadian Grand Prix despite chequered flag mix-up|url=http://www.theguardian.com/sport/2018/jun/10/sebastian-vettel-wins-canadian-grand-prix-f1-championship-lewis-hamilton|access-date=19 January 2022|website=The Guardian|archive-date=16 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220116233202/https://www.theguardian.com/sport/2018/jun/10/sebastian-vettel-wins-canadian-grand-prix-f1-championship-lewis-hamilton|url-status=live}}</ref> The following race in [[2018 French Grand Prix|France]] brought him second place.<ref>{{Cite web|title=F1 Results – 2018 French Grand Prix|date=23 June 2018 |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/2018/french-grand-prix/results|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=BBC Sport|archive-date=19 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220119232007/https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/2018/french-grand-prix/results|url-status=live}}</ref> In [[2018 Austrian Grand Prix|Austria]]—Red Bull's home track, he started fourth on the grid, passed Kimi Räikkönen before taking advantage of retirement from [[Valtteri Bottas]] and a botched pit-stop strategy by Lewis Hamilton, who later had to retire from fourth place, to claim the fourth race victory of his career.<ref>{{Citation|title=2018 Austrian Grand Prix: Race Highlights| date=July 2018 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JNmfS9PSeYg|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=19 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220119232005/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JNmfS9PSeYg|url-status=live}}</ref> In [[2018 British Grand Prix|Britain]], Verstappen was plagued by issues, finishing the first practice session early due to a gearbox problem and crashing in the second practice session before retiring from the race due to a brake problem.<ref>{{Cite web|date=8 July 2018|title=F1: The reason Max Verstappen spun then retired from the British GP|url=https://www.foxsports.com.au/motorsport/formula-one/f1-the-reason-max-verstappen-spun-then-retired-from-the-british-gp/news-story/bf90c9db2308a1b68936d240a7549c95|access-date=19 January 2022|work=Fox Sports|archive-date=19 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220119232005/https://www.foxsports.com.au/motorsport/formula-one/f1-the-reason-max-verstappen-spun-then-retired-from-the-british-gp/news-story/bf90c9db2308a1b68936d240a7549c95|url-status=live}}</ref> He would then finish fourth in [[2018 German Grand Prix|Germany]] after strategy errors let a recovering Hamilton get past him as he went on to win the race.<ref>{{Citation|title=2018 German Grand Prix: Race Highlights| date=22 July 2018 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gp-i8vtgzJE|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=13 October 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221013203408/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gp-i8vtgzJE|url-status=live}}</ref> Verstappen ended the first half of the season with a retirement in [[2018 Hungarian Grand Prix|Hungary]] and was narrowly behind his teammate in the championship due to his own recent resurgence and Ricciardo's unreliability.<ref>{{Cite web|title=Ricciardo: 'Weird' reliability inexplicable|url=https://www.motorsport.com/f1/news/ricciardo-weird-reliability-inexplicable/3202443/|access-date=19 January 2022|website=Motorsport.com|date=26 October 2018 |archive-date=24 April 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220424225311/https://www.motorsport.com/f1/news/ricciardo-weird-reliability-inexplicable/3202443/|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|title=RACE: Verstappen retires early in Hungary|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/video.race-verstappen-retires-early-in-hungary.6060943592001.html|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=Formula One|archive-date=10 March 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220310022859/https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/video.race-verstappen-retires-early-in-hungary.6060943592001.html|url-status=live}}</ref>

Verstappen enjoyed a very strong second half of the season, achieving podium finishes in [[2018 Belgian Grand Prix|Belgium]], [[2018 Singapore Grand Prix|Singapore]], [[2018 Japanese Grand Prix|Japan]] and the [[2018 United States Grand Prix|United States]], the latter of which he achieved second place having started from 18th on the grid due to a suspension failure in qualifying.<ref>{{Citation|title=2018 United States Grand Prix: Race Highlights| date=21 October 2018 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PNwPpqaLE60|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=19 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220119232524/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PNwPpqaLE60|url-status=live}}</ref> Following the qualifying session at the [[2018 Mexican Grand Prix|Mexican Grand Prix]], Verstappen revealed that a mechanical problem with his Red Bull under braking cost him any chance of becoming the youngest-ever Formula One pole-sitter. As a result, Ricciardo beat Verstappen to pole position by just 0.026 seconds.<ref>{{Citation|title=2018 Mexican Grand Prix: Qualifying Highlights| date=27 October 2018 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SVTDYyXP7E0|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=19 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220119233750/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SVTDYyXP7E0|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|title=Max Verstappen unhappy after missing out on history-making pole position|url=https://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12433/11533294/max-verstappen-unhappy-after-missing-out-on-history-making-pole-position|access-date=12 February 2022|publisher=Sky Sports |archive-date=12 February 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220212120158/https://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12433/11533294/max-verstappen-unhappy-after-missing-out-on-history-making-pole-position|url-status=live}}</ref> Verstappen had a better start than Ricciardo and took the lead of the race into the first corner, overtaking the fast-starting Mercedes of Lewis Hamilton. Verstappen earned his fifth career win in Mexico.<ref>{{Citation|title=2018 Mexican Grand Prix: Race Highlights| date=28 October 2018 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d1-VK12FZhs|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=19 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220119233748/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d1-VK12FZhs|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|title=Mexican Grand Prix 2018 report – Verstappen wins in Mexico as Hamilton takes fifth title|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.f1-2018-mexican-grand-prix-report.43bcuMZV3i8yk2a8kQw06w.html|access-date=17 February 2022|publisher=Formula One |archive-date=29 October 2018|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20181029014936/https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.f1-2018-mexican-grand-prix-report.43bcuMZV3i8yk2a8kQw06w.html|url-status=live}}</ref> He was poised to win the [[2018 Brazilian Grand Prix]], having overtaken Räikkönen, Vettel, Bottas, and Hamilton. However, he collided with [[Force India]] driver [[Esteban Ocon]] who was trying to unlap himself on faster tyres.<ref>{{Citation|title=Verstappen And Ocon Collide {{!}} 2018 Brazilian Grand Prix| date=12 November 2018 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6LSAaVq4Dsw|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=19 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220119233744/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6LSAaVq4Dsw|url-status=live}}</ref> Ocon received a 10-second stop-and-go penalty for the incident. After the collision with Ocon, Verstappen finished in second place behind Hamilton. During an argument with Ocon after the race, Verstappen pushed the Force India driver, for which he was given two days of "public service" as a penalty by the FIA.<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/139986/verstappen-given-public-service-for-ocon-shove|title=FIA gives Max Verstappen 'public service' for Esteban Ocon shove|last=Freeman|first=Glenn|website=Autosport|access-date=12 November 2018|archive-date=11 November 2018|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20181111214724/https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/139986/verstappen-given-public-service-for-ocon-shove|url-status=live}}</ref> He then finished his season with another podium as he finished third in Abu Dhabi.<ref>{{cite web |url=https://www.formula1.com/en/results.html/2018/races/999/abu-dhabi/race-result.html |title=Formula 1 2018 Etihad Airways Abu Dhabi Grand Prix – Race Result |publisher=Formula One |date=25 November 2018 |access-date=25 November 2018 |archive-date=13 January 2021 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210113155249/https://www.formula1.com/en/results.html/2018/races/999/abu-dhabi/race-result.html |url-status=live}}</ref>

Verstappen ended the season in fourth place in the championship with 249 points, claiming two wins, eleven podium finishes, and two fastest laps.<ref>{{cite web|title=Max Verstappen in 2018|url=https://www.racing-statistics.com/en/drivers/compare/max-verstappen/seasons/2018|access-date=19 December 2021|website=Formula 1 Statistics|archive-date=19 December 2021|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20211219093227/https://www.racing-statistics.com/en/drivers/compare/max-verstappen/seasons/2018|url-status=live}}</ref>

====2019–2020: Dominance over teammates and championshhip top 3 finishes====
[[File:Max Verstappen during Hungarian Formula 1 GP.jpg|thumb|Verstappen at the [[2019 Hungarian Grand Prix]], where he started on pole position for the first time in his career]]
In [[2019 Formula One World Championship|2019]] Red Bull switched from Renault to [[Honda in Formula One|Honda]] power units.<ref>{{Cite web|title=Red Bull agree deal to run Honda engine technology until 2025|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.breaking-red-bull-agree-deal-to-run-honda-engine-technology-until-2025.3wmTSMrtsv6miuwqApz8IT.html|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=Formula One|archive-date=15 February 2021|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210215215037/https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.breaking-red-bull-agree-deal-to-run-honda-engine-technology-until-2025.3wmTSMrtsv6miuwqApz8IT.html|url-status=live}}</ref> After Ricciardo moved to the [[Renault in Formula One|Renault]] team for 2019, Verstappen was joined at Red Bull by [[Pierre Gasly]].<ref>{{Cite web|title=Pierre Gasly joins Red Bull for 2019|work=Sky Sports |url=https://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12433/11477951/pierre-gasly-joins-red-bull-for-f1-2019|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=5 March 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220305224221/https://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12433/11477951/pierre-gasly-joins-red-bull-for-f1-2019|url-status=live}}</ref> Verstappen qualified in fourth and finished third in [[2019 Australian Grand Prix|Australia]], the first podium finish for a [[Honda in Formula One|Honda]]-powered driver since the [[2008 British Grand Prix]].<ref>{{Cite web|date=17 March 2019|title=F1 Australian Grand Prix 2019: Max Verstappen sends ominous Red Bull message after Honda milestone|url=https://www.foxsports.com.au/motorsport/formula-one/f1-australian-grand-prix-2019-max-verstappen-race-results-red-bull-honda-renault-video-latest-news/news-story/9d6d29140487e11eacfa8296e1f95f22|access-date=11 October 2021|work=Fox Sports|archive-date=28 October 2021|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20211028172923/https://www.foxsports.com.au/motorsport/formula-one/f1-australian-grand-prix-2019-max-verstappen-race-results-red-bull-honda-renault-video-latest-news/news-story/9d6d29140487e11eacfa8296e1f95f22|url-status=live}}</ref> Verstappen was on course for a second third-place finish in [[2019 Bahrain Grand Prix|Bahrain]] before a late safety car prevented him from overtaking Charles Leclerc's ailing Ferrari, keeping him in fourth place.<ref>{{Citation|title=2019 Bahrain Grand Prix: Race Highlights| date=31 March 2019 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0VjWY--QdMA|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=24 April 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220424225323/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0VjWY--QdMA|url-status=live}}</ref> Two more fourth-place finishes followed in [[2019 Chinese Grand Prix|China]] and [[2019 Azerbaijan Grand Prix|Azerbaijan]], and a podium in [[2019 Spanish Grand Prix|Spain]] in third place.<ref>{{Cite web|title=F1 Results – 2019 Spanish Grand Prix|date=11 May 2019 |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/2019/spanish-grand-prix/results|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=BBC Sport|archive-date=24 April 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220424225314/https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/2019/spanish-grand-prix/results|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|title=F1 Results – 2019 Azerbaijan Grand Prix|date=27 April 2019 |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/2019/azerbaijan-grand-prix/results/race|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=BBC Sport|archive-date=24 April 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220424225311/https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/2019/azerbaijan-grand-prix/results/race|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|title=F1 Results – 2019 Chinese Grand Prix|date=13 April 2019 |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/2019/chinese-grand-prix/results|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=BBC Sport|archive-date=13 October 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221013203417/https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/2019/chinese-grand-prix/results|url-status=live}}</ref> In [[2019 Monaco Grand Prix|Monaco]], Verstappen qualified in third place. He was released into the path of Valtteri Bottas during the drivers' pit stops, gaining second place but receiving a 5-second penalty as a result. Verstappen crossed the line in second place but was demoted to fourth by the penalty.<ref>{{Cite web|date=26 May 2019|title=Verstappen given penalty points for failing to avoid contact · RaceFans|url=https://www.racefans.net/2019/05/26/verstappen-given-penalty-points-for-failing-to-avoid-contact/|access-date=11 October 2021|website=RaceFans|archive-date=23 October 2021|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20211023170821/https://www.racefans.net/2019/05/26/verstappen-given-penalty-points-for-failing-to-avoid-contact/|url-status=live}}</ref>

In [[2019 Canadian Grand Prix|Canada]], Verstappen's final lap in the second qualifying session was hampered by a red flag brought out by [[Kevin Magnussen]]'s crash. This caused Verstappen to qualify 11th and start the race in ninth place.<ref>{{Cite web|date=8 June 2019|title=F1 Canadian Grand Prix 2019 qualifying results: Daniel Ricciardo stuns with best result since October as Sebastian Vettel snatches pole|url=https://www.foxsports.com.au/motorsport/formula-one/f1-canadian-grand-prix-2019-live-qualifying-results-timings-daniel-ricciardo-renault-lewis-hamilton-timesheets-updates-blog/news-story/95ac6b8933fff5af9a310a512de60366|url-status=live|access-date=11 January 2022|work=Fox Sports|archive-date=11 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220111041408/https://www.foxsports.com.au/motorsport/formula-one/f1-canadian-grand-prix-2019-live-qualifying-results-timings-daniel-ricciardo-renault-lewis-hamilton-timesheets-updates-blog/news-story/95ac6b8933fff5af9a310a512de60366}}</ref><ref>{{Cite 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220111041417/https://beyondtheflag.com/2019/06/09/formula-1-provisional-starting-grid-2019-canadian-grand-prix/}}</ref> He later recovered to finish fifth.<ref>{{Cite web|last=Richards|first=Giles|date=9 June 2019|title=Lewis Hamilton handed Canadian Grand Prix win after Vettel's penalty pain|url=http://www.theguardian.com/sport/2019/jun/09/lewis-hamilton-canadian-grand-prix-vettel-penalty|url-status=live|access-date=11 January 2022|website=The Guardian|archive-date=5 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220105160525/https://www.theguardian.com/sport/2019/jun/09/lewis-hamilton-canadian-grand-prix-vettel-penalty}}</ref> In [[2019 French Grand Prix|France]] he started and finished in fourth place.<ref>{{Cite web|title=French Grand Prix 2019 – F1 Race|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/racing/2019/France.html|url-status=live|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=Formula One|archive-date=28 October 2014|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20141028202800/https://www.formula1.com/en/racing/2019/France.html}}</ref> In [[2019 Austrian Grand Prix|Austria]], Verstappen started third but suffered a poor start, dropping down to eighth. After a charge towards the front, he made his way up to second before controversially passing Leclerc for the lead of the race with three laps to go.<ref>{{Citation|title=Verstappen's Stunning Fightback {{!}} 2019 Austrian Grand Prix| date=30 June 2019 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=cV0G1zN8kJU|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=13 October 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221013203250/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=cV0G1zN8kJU|url-status=live}}</ref> This marked the first Honda-powered race victory since the [[2006 Hungarian Grand Prix]].<ref>{{Cite news|last1=McVeigh (earlier)|first1=Niall|last2=Unwin (now)|first2=Will|date=30 June 2019|title=F1: Verstappen's win stands after Austrian Grand Prix stewards inquiry – as it happened|work=The Guardian|url=https://www.theguardian.com/sport/live/2019/jun/30/f1-formula-one-austrian-grand-prix-live|access-date=11 January 2022|issn=0261-3077|archive-date=11 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220111041738/https://www.theguardian.com/sport/live/2019/jun/30/f1-formula-one-austrian-grand-prix-live|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|date=1 July 2019|title=Honda hails new beginning after first F1 win since 2006|url=https://www.autoblog.com/2019/07/01/honda-hail-new-beginning-after-first-f1-win-since-2006/|url-status=live|access-date=11 January 2022|website=Autoblog|archive-date=13 October 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221013203242/https://www.autoblog.com/2019/07/01/honda-hail-new-beginning-after-first-f1-win-since-2006/}}</ref> In [[2019 British Grand Prix|Britain]], Verstappen, running in third place,<ref>{{Cite web|last=Simpson|first=Christopher|date=12 July 2019|title=F1 British Grand Prix 2019 Qualifying: Results, Times from Friday's Practice|url=https://bleacherreport.com/articles/2845285-british-f1-grand-prix-2019-qualifying-results-times-from-fridays-practice|url-status=live|access-date=11 January 2022|website=Bleacher behind during an overtake attempt by Sebastian Vettel and spun into the gravel. Verstappen was able to continue and crossed the line in fifth place.<ref>{{Cite web|date=14 July 2019|title=British Grand Prix 2019 race report: Hamilton victorious as Verstappen and Vettel collide in Silverstone thriller|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.hamilton-victorious-as-verstappen-and-vettel-collide-in-silverstone.3yHjP4nXKPkt4HYJQ7Bagt.html|url-status=live|access-date=11 January 2022|publisher=Formula One|archive-date=14 July 2019|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20190714153942/https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.hamilton-victorious-as-verstappen-and-vettel-collide-in-silverstone.3yHjP4nXKPkt4HYJQ7Bagt.html}}</ref>

The wet and chaotic [[2019 German Grand Prix|German Grand Prix]] began similarly to the race in Austria for Verstappen, as a poor start caused him to fall behind. However, he would inherit the lead midway through the race after a crash by race leader Hamilton.<ref>{{Citation|title=2019 German Grand Prix: Race Highlights| date=28 July 2019 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RYHQmBULhLc|access-date=19 January 2022|archive-date=19 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220119235641/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RYHQmBULhLc|url-status=live}}</ref> Verstappen would go on to extend his lead after the track began to dry, claiming his second victory of the season.<ref>{{Cite web|last=Richards|first=Giles|date=28 July 2019|title=Max Verstappen wins thrilling German GP after Lewis Hamilton error|url=http://www.theguardian.com/sport/2019/jul/28/max-verstappen-wins-german-grand-prix-hamilton-error|url-status=live|access-date=29 December 2021|website=The Guardian|archive-date=15 November 2021|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20211115040807/https://www.theguardian.com/sport/2019/jul/28/max-verstappen-wins-german-grand-prix-hamilton-error}}</ref> In [[2019 Hungarian Grand Prix|Hungary]], he claimed his maiden [[List of Formula One polesitters|pole position]]—becoming the [[Formula One drivers from the Netherlands|first Dutch driver]] to do so—and led most of the race before being passed in the closing laps by Hamilton, who had made another stop for fresh tyres in a gamble to catch the leader.<ref>{{cite web |date=3 August 2019 |title=Max Verstappen pakt eerste pole position uit carrière |trans-title=Max Verstappen takes first career pole position |url=https://www.hartvannederland.nl/nieuws/2019/max-verstappen-pakt-eerste-pole-position-uit-carriere/ |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20190803222328/https://www.hartvannederland.nl/nieuws/2019/max-verstappen-pakt-eerste-pole-position-uit-carriere/ |archive-date=3 August 2019 |access-date=4 August 2019 |publisher=[[:nl:Hart van Nederland]] |language=nl}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web|date=4 August 2019|title=Hungarian Grand Prix 2019 F1 race report: Hamilton brilliantly hunts down Verstappen to snatch Hungary victory|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.hamilton-brilliantly-hunts-down-verstappen-to-snatch-hungary-victory.4QPgPYGdCXqeEirMjUEhZU.html|url-status=live|access-date=29 December 2021|publisher=Formula One|archive-date=29 December web|title=Verstappen takes maiden career pole in thrilling Hungary qualifying|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.verstappen-takes-maiden-career-pole-in-thrilling-hungary-qualifying.5AABsomaDmZaBusQxjCZuP.html|access-date=19 January 2022|publisher=Formula One|archive-date=18 January
