|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.motorsport.com/f1/news/max-verstappen-explains-dutch-gp-near-miss-i-tried-to-send-it/10755135 |title=Max Verstappen explains Dutch GP near-miss: "I tried to send it" |last=Harrington |first=Alex |date=31 August 2025 |website=[[Motorsport.com]] |publisher=[[Motorsport Network]] |access-date=5 September 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250906160704/https://www.motorsport.com/f1/news/max-verstappen-explains-dutch-gp-near-miss-i-tried-to-send-it/10755135/ |archive-date=6 September 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref> His pole lap in [[2025 Italian Grand Prix|Italy]] was the [[List of Formula One race records|fastest in Formula One history]],<ref>{{Cite news |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/formula1/articles/ce86v83j1r2o |title=How Verstappen surpassed fastest F1 cars in history |last=Benson |first=Andrew |date=6 September 2025 |work=[[BBC Sport]] |publisher=[[BBC]] |access-date=7 September 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250907041532/https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/articles/ce86v83j1r2o |archive-date=7 September 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref> where he overcame early opposition from Norris to win the Grand Prix by over 19 seconds in record speed.<ref>{{Cite news |url=https://www.nytimes.com/athletic/6609867/2025/09/08/max-verstappen-red-bull-f1-italian-gp |title=How Red Bull's tactical F1 shift fueled Max Verstappen's commanding Monza win |last=Coleman |first=Madeline |date=8 September 2025 |work=[[The Athletic]] |publisher=[[The New York Times]] |access-date=8 September 2025 |issn=1553-8095 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250908162940/https://www.nytimes.com/athletic/6609867/2025/09/08/max-verstappen-red-bull-f1-italian-gp |archive-date=8 September 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref> He claimed a ''[[Glossary of motorsport terms#G|grand chelem]]'' with his 14-second victory at the {{F1GP|2025|Azerbaijan}}, before finishing second to Russell in [[2025 Singapore Grand Prix|Singapore]]. His back-to-back victories in the [[2025 United States Grand Prix|United States]] sprint and Grand Prix reduced his deficit to 40 points. He recovered from fifth to third in [[2025 Mexico City Grand Prix|Mexico City]] with a [[Glossary of motorsport terms#0–9|one-stop strategy]]. Finishing fourth from sixth at the [[2025 São Paulo Grand Prix|São Paulo]] sprint, Verstappen qualified sixteenth for the main race, his lowest qualifying position - excluding any penalties - since the [[2021 Russian Grand Prix]], which he chose not to run any qualifying laps for due to impending engine and grid penalties. Red Bull started him from the pit lane after changing his engine in ''[[parc fermé]]''; surviving an early puncture, he climbed up to third in the race to draw ever closer to the championship lead. Verstappen won at [[2025 Las Vegas Grand Prix|Las Vegas]] with both McLarens being disqualified post-race. This moved him level on points with Piastri and 24 behind championship leader Norris. Verstappen finished fourth in the [[2025 Qatar Grand Prix|Qatar sprint]] before improving to collect a win in the main race, bringing him twelve points behind Norris and overtaking Piastri for second in the championship. Max won the final race of the season, placing him merely two points behind the  new world champion, Lando Norris.

==Driver profile==

===Qualifying pace and consistency===
[[File:2018 Chinese Grand Prix Qualifying Max Verstappen (27842985498).jpg|thumb|left|Verstappen on a qualifying lap at the [[2018 Chinese Grand Prix]]]]
Following Verstappen's maiden pole position at the 2019 Hungarian Grand Prix, former Formula One World Champion Nico Rosberg described him as the fastest driver in Formula One. Rosberg elucidated that Verstappen did not have the fastest car during the qualifying session in Hungary, emphasising that it was Verstappen's raw speed that allowed him to secure pole position: "Mercedes still had the fastest car, and it's just Verstappen with his driving that put it up there in pole."<ref>{{Citation|title=5 reasons why Verstappen is currently the fastest F1 driver {{!}} Hungarian GP Qualifying Analysis| date=3 August 2019 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VLGlqQOhZ2Y|access-date=7 January 2022|archive-date=13 October 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221013205356/https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VLGlqQOhZ2Y|url-status=live}}</ref>

In 2019, former Formula One World Champion [[Jenson Button]] stated that he believes Verstappen is the fastest Formula One driver ever: "I think he is the fastest driver that has ever driven an F1 car. I really do, I think he is unbelievably fast."<ref>{{Cite web|date=17 October 2019|title=Button says Verstappen is the fastest F1 driver ever|url=https://www.espn.com/f1/story/_/id/27867007/verstappen-fastest-f1-driver-ever|url-status=live|publisher=ESPN|access-date=7 January 2022|archive-date=8 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220108224915/https://www.espn.com/f1/story/_/id/27867007/verstappen-fastest-f1-driver-ever}}</ref> Journalist Scott Mitchell from ''The Race'' commented: "When everything comes together, Verstappen is the quickest driver in F1 over one lap, but the odd mistake and scruffy session must be eliminated."<ref>{{cite web|date=10 February 2020|title=Ranking Leclerc and Verstappen in the fight to topple Hamilton|url=https://the-race.com/formula-1/ranking-leclerc-and-verstappen-in-the-fight-to-topple-hamilton/|access-date=8 January 2022|website=The Race|archive-date=8 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220108192416/https://the-race.com/formula-1/ranking-leclerc-and-verstappen-in-the-fight-to-topple-hamilton/|url-status=live}}</ref> Helmut Marko, the advisor to the [[Red Bull GmbH]] Formula One teams, stated that Verstappen is the fastest driver Red Bull have ever had.<ref>{{cite web |title=Max Verstappen looking set for F1 2020 at Red Bull |url=https://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12433/11775650/max-verstappen-looking-set-for-f1-2020-at-red-bull |access-date=18 August 2022 |publisher=Sky Sports |archive-date=18 August 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220818170804/https://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12433/11775650/max-verstappen-looking-set-for-f1-2020-at-red-bull |url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{cite web |title=Marko rates the best and worst traits of Red Bull's F1 stars |url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/marko-rates-the-best-and-worst-traits-of-red-bulls-f1-stars/10334712/ |access-date=18 August 2022 |website=Autosport |date=8 July 2022 |archive-date=18 August 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220818170804/https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/marko-rates-the-best-and-worst-traits-of-red-bulls-f1-stars/10334712/amp/ |url-status=live}}</ref>

In 2021, three-time Formula One World Champion [[Jackie Stewart]] claimed that Verstappen is the fastest driver in Formula One, but he also added that Verstappen is still in the "puppy stage" of his career.<ref>{{Cite web|date=16 September 2021|title=Verstappen now 'fastest driver in the business', Jackie Stewart claims|url=https://www.independent.co.uk/sport/motor-racing/formula1/max-verstappen-lewis-hamilton-jackie-stewart-f1-b1921212.html |archive-url=https://ghostarchive.org/archive/20220512/https://www.independent.co.uk/sport/motor-racing/formula1/max-verstappen-lewis-hamilton-jackie-stewart-f1-b1921212.html |archive-date=12 May 2022 |url-access=subscription |url-status=live|access-date=7 January 2022|website=The Independent}}</ref> [[Peter Windsor]], an experienced Formula One journalist, expressed Verstappen's ability to change direction "quicker than anybody else" through the high-speed section Maggots and Becketts: Silverstone's fastest sequence of corners. Windsor stressed that Verstappen's lightning-quick change of direction is a result of the innate feel he has for creating the "perfect platform" to achieve what he wants with the car during a lap. He is intrinsically able to create, for "a trillionth of a second", a minuscule "flat area" between the left and the right of a change of direction ahead of him, in order to attain a stable balance with the car before applying the next steering and throttle input.<ref>{{Citation|title=If Max is an early braker why is he so fast? By Peter Windsor| date=22 July 2021 |url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=M7AgJXQ2rEI|access-date=13 January 2022|archive-date=11 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220111082058/https://www.youtube.com/watch?app=desktop&v=M7AgJXQ2rEI|url-status=live}}</ref> [[Scuderia AlphaTauri]]'s team principal, [[Franz Tost]], declared Verstappen to be the fastest driver in Formula One; following the Dutchman's "really deserved" maiden title triumph in 2021.<ref name="Woodhouse2021">{{cite news |last=Woodhouse |first=Jamie |title=Franz Tost: 'Fastest driver' Max Verstappen deserved his Formula 1 world title |url=https://www.planetf1.com/news/franz-tost-max-verstappen-deserved-title |access-date=25 October 2023 |work=PlanetF1 |date=14 December 2021 |archive-date=6 December 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221206183802/https://www.planetf1.com/news/franz-tost-max-verstappen-deserved-title/ |url-status=live}}</ref> After winning the {{F1|2024}} World Drivers' Championship in the third-placed [[Red Bull RB20|RB20]], James Elson of ''[[Motor Sport (magazine)|Motor Sport]]'' stated "if there was any doubt beforehand, 2024 proved that Max Verstappen is F1's best driver", adding that "not only the fastest, capable of creating one-lap magic, he knows how to produce all it takes to win in the race too".<ref>{{Cite magazine |url=https://www.motorsportmagazine.com/articles/single-seaters/f1/2024-f1-drivers-ranked-is-verstappen-really-no1/ |title=2024 F1 drivers ranked: Is Verstappen really No1? |last=Elson |first=James |date=10 December 2024 |magazine=[[Motor Sport (magazine)|Motor Sport]] |access-date=10 December 2024 |issn=0027-2019}}</ref>

Verstappen received widespread acclaim for his surprise pole lap at the [[2025 Japanese Grand Prix]],<ref name="JPN2025"/> with Andrew Benson of ''[[BBC Sport]]'' commenting that "many F1 observers regarded [Verstappen's qualifying lap] as one of the greatest of all time".<ref>{{Cite web |last=Benson |first=Andrew |date=14 April 2025 |title=Max Verstappen: Red Bull adviser Helmut Marko has 'great concern' about world champion's future with team |url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/articles/c9dj0elnexyo |access-date=14 April 2025 |website=BBC Sport |language=en-GB}}</ref>

===Dominance over teammates===
[[File:Red Bull duo 2016 Malaysia Race.jpg|right|thumb|Verstappen ''(right)'' was teammates with [[Daniel Ricciardo]] ''(left)'' from {{F1|2016}} to {{F1|2018}}, pictured at the [[2016 Malaysian Grand Prix]].]]
Former Formula One driver [[Eddie Irvine]] praised Verstappen, stating that he is "by far the most dominant team leader on the grid." Irvine highlighted that Verstappen "has had many different second drivers in the team and none of them have got close to him."<ref name="Sharan2021Irvine">{{cite news |last=Sharan |first=Rahul |title=Irvine finds similarities between Verstappen and Schumacher |url=https://racingnews365.com/irvine-finds-similarities-between-verstappen-and-schumacher |access-date=25 October 2023 |work=RacingNews365 |date=10 June 2021 |archive-date=14 June 2021 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210614105847/https://racingnews365.com/irvine-finds-similarities-between-verstappen-and-schumacher |url-status=live}}</ref> During the 2020 season, Jenson Button stated that he does not "think there's been a driver that's annihilated their teammates like he [Verstappen] has in a very long time."<ref>{{cite web|title=Button: Verstappen &quot;annihilating&quot; team-mates like Senna|url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/button-verstappen-annihilating-team-mates-like-senna-4979817/4979817/|access-date=13 January 2022|website=Autosport|date=25 August 2020 |archive-date=13 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220113084508/https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/button-verstappen-annihilating-team-mates-like-senna-4979817/4979817/|url-status=live}}</ref> Motorsport columnist and former Formula One driver [[Jolyon Palmer]], commented that Verstappen's maiden World Championship triumph could usher in a "new era of dominance" in the sport. Palmer wrote:<blockquote>[Verstappen's] qualifying pace is frightening and has been the basis for this title-charging campaign. He's had the most pole positions of anyone with 10 – almost half of the races, and it would have been half had he completed his mesmeric Jeddah lap, which ended up being possibly Max's biggest error in a near-perfect season. For a driver who has demolished three successive team mates in three years and made only a handful of small mistakes in that time, you question what scope {{sic|their}}  even is for improvement – but if he can take his game onto another level from here, we could be about to see the start of a new era of dominance.<ref>{{Cite web|title=PALMER: Whatever you thought of the Abu Dhabi outcome, Verstappen was a worthy champion in 2021|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.palmer-whatever-you-thought-of-the-abu-dhabi-outcome-verstappen-was-a-worthy.6RkHgJTJgx8HjlqwPraGo0.html|access-date=2022-01-27|publisher=Formula One |archive-date=13 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220113090926/https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.palmer-whatever-you-thought-of-the-abu-dhabi-outcome-verstappen-was-a-worthy.6RkHgJTJgx8HjlqwPraGo0.html|url-status=live}}</ref></blockquote>Journalist Scott Mitchell expounded the reason why the Dutchman's teammates have fallen short "even with the data". Mitchell wrote: "Verstappen drives mainly on intuition, and that comes from years of training and preparation. He has a database in his head which he can use immediately. Even with data, Gasly and Albon could see where he was faster, but they could never grasp how he was faster there. That's because what makes Verstappen so good is mainly unconscious." According to Mitchell, those years of training and driving on his intuition has made it impossible for other drivers to measure up to Verstappen.<ref>{{Cite web|date=19 January 2021|title=Verstappen 'driving style' myth is a trait of greatness|url=https://the-race.com/formula-1/verstappen-driving-style-myth-is-a-trait-of-greatness/|access-date=13 January 2022|website=The Race|archive-date=15 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220115064106/https://the-race.com/formula-1/verstappen-driving-style-myth-is-a-trait-of-greatness/|url-status=live}}</ref>

[[File:Verstappen & Perez Chinese GP 2024.jpg|left|thumb|Verstappen ''(right)'' was teammates with [[Sergio Pérez]] ''(left)'' from 2021 to 2024, pictured at the [[2024 Chinese Grand Prix]].]]
Helmut Marko asserted that Verstappen's progress has moved him clear of his former Formula 1 team-mate Daniel Ricciardo, in both qualifying and race trim. "There is specific data," Marko told ''Autosport'', "In qualifying the gap to Ricciardo is greater. The strange thing is Max sometimes slides more, but still manages to keep the tyres alive." Verstappen previously mentioned that he was three or four-tenths faster on average than "very fast qualifier" Daniel Ricciardo.<ref>{{Cite web|last=GMM|date=28 September 2017|title=Max Verstappen says he's considerably faster than Red Bull F1 teammate Daniel Ricciardo|url=http://autoweek.com/racing/formula-1/a1831511/max-verstappen-says-hes-considerably-faster-red-bull-f1-teammate-daniel/|access-date=15 January 2022|website=Autoweek|archive-date=15 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220115065822/https://www.autoweek.com/racing/formula-1/a1831511/max-verstappen-says-hes-considerably-faster-red-bull-f1-teammate-daniel/|url-status=live}}</ref> Marko concluded that then 20-year-old Verstappen "clearly moved away" from Ricciardo since the "second half of the 2018 season."<ref>{{Cite web |last1=Nimmervoll  |first1=Christian  |last2=Mitchell |first2=Scott  |date=13 January 2019 |title=Marko: Verstappen moved clear of ex-Red Bull F1 team-mate Ricciardo |url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/marko-verstappen-moved-clear-of-ex-red-bull-f1-team-mate-ricciardo-5282660/5282660/ |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220115032851/https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/marko-verstappen-moved-clear-of-ex-red-bull-f1-team-mate-ricciardo-5282660/5282660/ |archive-date=15 January 2022 |access-date=15 January 2022 |website=Autosport}}</ref>

===Natural talent===
{{multiple image
| width             = 200
| direction         = vertical
| image1            = 2021 British Grand Prix (51349530573) (cropped).jpg

}}

Following his maiden Formula One win, at the [[2016 Spanish Grand Prix]], three-time World Drivers' Champion [[Niki Lauda]] described Verstappen as the "talent of the century."<ref>{{cite web|title=Niki Lauda calls Max Verstappen the 'talent of the century'|url=https://www.autoweek.com/racing/formula-1/a1846031/niki-lauda-calls-f1-driver-max-verstappen-talent-century/|website=autoweek.com|date=16 May 2016|access-date=28 January 2022|archive-date=28 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220128125836/https://www.autoweek.com/racing/formula-1/a1846031/niki-lauda-calls-f1-driver-max-verstappen-talent-century/|url-status=live}}</ref> Red Bull team principal Christian Horner stated that Verstappen is the best driver Red Bull have ever seen: "I have no doubt [Verstappen is] the best we have seen on one of our cars, in terms of outright raw ability and commitment. He's the best driver we've seen."<ref>{{Cite web |title=Horner on the 'rare' quality Verstappen shares with Hamilton |url=https://racingnews365.com/horner-on-the-rare-quality-verstappen-shares-with-hamilton |access-date=18 August 2022 |website=RacingNews365 |date=10 January 2022 |archive-date=18 August 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220818175204/https://racingnews365.com/horner-on-the-rare-quality-verstappen-shares-with-hamilton |url-status=live}}</ref>

[[Gerhard Berger]] echoed {{F1|2009}} World Drivers' Champion [[Jenson Button]]'s comments, anointing Max Verstappen as the Formula One driver of this era with the most raw talent. Speaking to ''Sport1'', Berger ventured: "When it comes to raw talent, Max Verstappen is the most talented of all. But it's not just about raw talent, it's about the whole package. That means it's not just about setting the fastest laps but also to win a race. And not just [about] one race but many and eventually a championship, and championships."<ref>{{Cite web|date=15 June 2021|title=Berger: Verstappen probably has most raw talent of all|url=https://www.grandprix247.com/2021/06/15/gerhard-berger-says-when-it-comes-to-raw-talent-max-verstappen-is-probably-on-top-of-it-all/|access-date=8 January 2022|website=GRAND PRIX 247|archive-date=8 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220108010013/https://www.grandprix247.com/2021/06/15/gerhard-berger-says-when-it-comes-to-raw-talent-max-verstappen-is-probably-on-top-of-it-all/|url-status=live}}</ref>

Two-time World Drivers' Champion [[Fernando Alonso]] commented that Verstappen is the standout driver in Formula One, declaring him to be "one step ahead" of everyone. Alonso noted that Verstappen deserved to win the [[2021 Formula One World Championship]], ahead of the title decider that saw Verstappen and [[Lewis Hamilton]] enter level on points: "Mercedes lately have been more performing and they've won a couple of races now, but Max is driving—in my opinion—one step ahead of all of us. We saw the [qualifying] lap in Jeddah, until he touched the wall at the last corner, that lap was coming from Max, not the Red Bull."<ref>{{Cite web|title=Alonso believes Verstappen 'driving one step ahead of everyone' as he says Dutchman 'deserves' 2021 title|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.alonso-believes-verstappen-driving-one-step-ahead-of-everyone-as-he-says.2j28pfrHJJlpCfuykTmw0T.html|access-date=12 January 2022|publisher=Formula One|archive-date=12 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220112054056/https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.alonso-believes-verstappen-driving-one-step-ahead-of-everyone-as-he-says.2j28pfrHJJlpCfuykTmw0T.html|url-status=live}}</ref> [[Karun Chandhok]] likened Verstappen's ruthless and uncompromising attitude in race combat to seven-time World Drivers' Champion [[Michael Schumacher]].<ref>{{cite news |last=Chandhok |first=Karun |title=Karun Chandhok's top 10 Formula 1 drivers of the 2021 season: Who makes the cut after epic year? |url=https://www.skysports.com/f1/news/36347/12498220/karun-chandhoks-top-10-formula-1-drivers-of-the-2021-season-who-makes-the-cut-after-epic-year |publisher=Sky Sports |access-date=18 December 2021 |archive-date=18 December 2021 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20211218102740/https://www.skysports.com/f1/news/36347/12498220/karun-chandhoks-top-10-formula-1-drivers-of-the-2021-season-who-makes-the-cut-after-epic-year |url-status=live}}</ref> By {{F1|2025}}, Andrew Benson of ''[[BBC Sport]]'' opined that Verstappen was "regarded by almost everyone in [Formula One] as the best driver in the world".<ref>{{Cite news |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/formula1/articles/ceq7z05ype7o |title=Horner's demise at Red Bull: what we know happened and why |last=Benson |first=Andrew |date=9 July 2025 |work=[[BBC Sport]] |publisher=[[BBC]] |access-date=9 July 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250709184511/https://www.bbc.com/sport/formula1/articles/ceq7z05ype7o |archive-date=9 July 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref>

===Racecraft===
[[File:Podium 2017 Malaysia.jpg|thumb|left|Verstappen ''(middle)'' on the podium with [[Lewis Hamilton]] ''(left)'' and [[Daniel Ricciardo]] ''(right)'' after winning the [[2017 Malaysian Grand Prix]]]]
Following Verstappen being given a warning for his driving conduct at the 2016 Belgian Grand Prix, the 18-year-old received the backing of then three-time champion Lewis Hamilton, who commented: "firstly, give the guy a break, he is 18 years old. What the frickin' heck were any of us doing at 18?"<ref>{{cite web |title=Verstappen Welcomes Support From Hamilton |url=https://news.sky.com/story/verstappen-welcomes-support-from-hamilton-10567203 |publisher=Sky News |date=6 September 2016 |access-date=12 January 2022 |archive-date=5 February 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220205015109/https://news.sky.com/story/verstappen-welcomes-support-from-hamilton-10567203 |url-status=live}}</ref>

Despite earning high praise and acclaim from Formula One drivers and experts in and out of the sport, Verstappen faced criticism in the wake of the penalties he incurred during the [[2021 Saudi Arabian Grand Prix]]. Following this race, former Formula One driver [[Martin Brundle]] wrote: "Such is [his] car control and cunning, he's sometimes able to pull off the audacious moves and leave a margin of doubt as to whether it's hard racing or simply a professional foul outside of the regulations and it's those moments which are generating the confusion, controversies and inconsistencies. [[Ayrton Senna]] and Michael Schumacher had their faults too, and I was on the receiving end from both of them on occasions, but it's a sizeable dent on their immense reputations, not a positive."<ref>{{Cite web|title=Martin Brundle: Max Verstappen must stop these tactics vs Lewis Hamilton for his legacy... but what a title showdown we'll have|url=https://www.skysports.com/f1/news/24096/12488713/martin-brundle-max-verstappen-must-stop-these-tactics-vs-lewis-hamilton-for-his-legacy-but-what-a-title-showdown-well-have|access-date=9 January 2022|publisher=Sky Sports|archive-date=9 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220109043216/https://www.skysports.com/f1/news/24096/12488713/martin-brundle-max-verstappen-must-stop-these-tactics-vs-lewis-hamilton-for-his-legacy-but-what-a-title-showdown-well-have|url-status=live}}</ref> Following an impressive first half of the 2022 season, journalist [[David Tremayne]] claimed Verstappen had elevated himself to a new higher level compared to his 2021 title winning season, suggesting Verstappen's first title had made him more relaxed and "chirpy".<ref>{{cite web |last1=Tremayne |first1=David |title=TREMAYNE: Why 2022 Max Verstappen is an upgrade on the 2021 version |url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.tremayne-why-2022-max-verstappen-is-an-upgrade-on-the-2021-version.6ajpJsL1vXqlNQMzTiTbtL.html |publisher=Formula One |access-date=4 August 2022 |archive-date=4 August 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220804135531/https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article.tremayne-why-2022-max-verstappen-is-an-upgrade-on-the-2021-version.6ajpJsL1vXqlNQMzTiTbtL.html |url-status=live}}</ref>

Following the [[2024 Mexico City Grand Prix]], Verstappen received widespread condemnation from fans and media for incidents involving title rival [[Lando Norris]], for which he received 20 seconds worth of time penalties.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article/explained-why-the-stewards-decided-to-hit-verstappen-with-not-one-but-two.6oI6P7n0TDUKUC9215E8JW|title=EXPLAINED: Why the stewards decided to hit Verstappen with not one but two penalties in Mexico|publisher=Formula One|date=28 October 2024|access-date=30 October 2024|first=Lawrence|last=Barretto}}</ref> Many commentators and former drivers likened his tactics and 'win-at-all-costs' attitude to those of Senna and Schumacher,<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.f1oversteer.com/news/the-michael-schumacher-advice-jos-verstappen-gave-to-max-after-the-mexico-city-grand-prix/|title=The Michael Schumacher advice Jos Verstappen gave to Max after the Mexico City Grand Prix|website=F1 Oversteer|date=30 October 2024|access-date=30 October 2024|first=Rory|last=Mitchell}}</ref><ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/racing/f1/story/_/id/42043242/to-f1-surprise-max-verstappen-overstepped-mexico-city-grand-prix|website=ESPN|date=27 October 2024|access-date=30 October 2024|first=Nate|last=Saunders|title=To few in F1's surprise, Verstappen overstepped in Mexico}}</ref> whilst {{F1|1996}} World Drivers' Champion [[Damon Hill]] characterised them as reminiscent of ''[[Wacky Races]]'' antagonist [[Dick Dastardly]].<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/formula1/articles/cvge8n9yzdlo|work=BBC Sport|date=30 October 2024|access-date=30 October 2024|title='Dick Dastardly' tactics – Hill criticises Verstappen|first=Bobbie|last=Jackson}}</ref> Before the following race in [[2024 São Paulo Grand Prix|São Paulo]], Verstappen criticised the alleged British bias in the media, stating "I have the wrong passport".<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/verstappen-i-have-the-wrong-passport-for-the-f1-paddock/10668853/|title=Verstappen: I have the wrong passport for the F1 paddock|first=Filip|last=Cleeren|date=1 November 2024|access-date=4 November 2024|website=Autosport}}</ref> Upon his victory from 17th on the grid in the rain-affected race, he stated in the post-race press conference "I appreciate all of you being here, but I don't see any British press. Do they have to run to the airport, or [do they not] know where the press conference is?"<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.independent.co.uk/f1/max-verstappen-f1-johnny-herbert-damon-hill-norris-b2640841.html|title=Max Verstappen calls out 'British press' after wet-weather masterclass in Brazil|first=Philip|last=Duncan|date=4 November 2024|access-date=4 November 2024|work=The Independent}}</ref>

===Fan support===
[[File:FIA F1 Austria 2021 Post-Race Scene.jpg|thumb|right|The Verstappen grandstand at the [[2021 Austrian Grand Prix]]]]
Verstappen has accumulated a significant fanbase from around the world, particularly from his home country of the Netherlands. The frequent association of Verstappen with the colour [[orange (colour)|orange]]—the [[national colour]] of the Netherlands—has led to his fanbase being referred to as the ''Orange Army''.<ref>{{cite news|url=https://www.theguardian.com/sport/2023/aug/28/f1-max-verstappen-will-not-forget-win-in-front-of-adoring-orange-army|work=The Guardian|date=28 August 2023|last=Richards|first=Giles|access-date=16 October 2024|title=Max Verstappen adds to highlight reel in front of his adoring orange army}}</ref><ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.gpblog.com/en/news/303938/max-verstappen-on-the-support-of-the-orange-army.html|website=GPBlog|date=29 September 2024|last=Crothall|first=Sophia|access-date=16 October 2024|title=Verstappen 'wouldn't wish pressure' from Orange Army on anyone}}</ref>

Several races across Europe have dedicated Verstappen [[grandstand]]s to accommodate thousands of travelling Dutch fans, boosting ticket sales at Grands Prix such as in [[Belgian Grand Prix|Belgium]], [[Austrian Grand Prix|Austria]] and [[Hungarian Grand Prix|Hungary]].<ref name="Smith 2018">{{cite web |last=Smith |first=Luke |title=The Max Factor: How Verstappen's fans have changed F1 |website=Crash |date=26 August 2018 |url=https://www.crash.net/f1/feature/904148/1/max-factor-how-verstappens-fans-have-changed-f1 |access-date=9 February 2022 |archive-date=9 February 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220209210247/https://www.crash.net/f1/feature/904148/1/max-factor-how-verstappens-fans-have-changed-f1 |url-status=live}}</ref> A Verstappen grandstand featured at the inaugural [[Las Vegas Grand Prix]] in [[2023 Las Vegas Grand Prix|2023]].<ref>{{Cite web|url=https://news.verstappen.com/en/article/5092/|website=Verstappen.com|date=11 May 2023|title=Exclusive Max Verstappen Grandstand at the Las Vegas Grand Prix 2023|last=Hendrix|first=Niels|access-date=16 October 2024}}</ref>

During the [[2021 Dutch Grand Prix]], the managing director of [[Honda in Formula One]], {{ill|Masashi Yamamoto (motorsport)|lt=Masashi Yamamoto|ja|山本雅史 (モータースポーツ)}}, praised Verstappen's support, stating that "in the six years I've been involved with Honda F1, I've never seen such a great turnout as at the Dutch Grand Prix. It was like being in a football stadium."<ref name="gpblog.com 2022">{{cite web |title=Honda impressed by Verstappen fans: 'Never seen such a great turnout' |website=gpblog.com |date=2 January 2022 |url=https://www.gpblog.com/en/news/102453/honda-impressed-by-verstappen-fans-never-seen-such-a-great-turnout.html |access-date=24 March 2022 |archive-date=23 February 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220223034800/https://www.gpblog.com/en/news/102453/honda-impressed-by-verstappen-fans-never-seen-such-a-great-turnout.html |url-status=live}}</ref>

In October 2021, Verstappen was voted the most popular Formula One driver in an official worldwide survey conducted by [[Nielsen Media Research]] and [[Motorsport Network]], earning 14.4% of the vote with particular success in the [[United States]] and [[Japan]].<ref name="Galloway 2021">{{cite web |last=Galloway |first=James |title=F1 fan survey lists Max Verstappen as most-popular driver ahead of Lando Norris and Lewis Hamilton |publisher=Sky Sports |date=21 October 2021 |url=https://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12433/12440256/f1-fan-survey-lists-max-verstappen-as-most-popular-driver-ahead-of-lando-norris-and-lewis-hamilton |access-date=24 March 2022 |archive-date=23 February 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220223012214/https://www.skysports.com/f1/news/12433/12440256/f1-fan-survey-lists-max-verstappen-as-most-popular-driver-ahead-of-lando-norris-and-lewis-hamilton |url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.formula1.com/en/latest/article/new-global-fan-survey-reveals-f1s-most-popular-team-anddriver.2evqTWEPsWYeBlw9vmSyBs|title=New global fan survey reveals Formula 1's most popular team and driver|publisher=Formula One|date=21 October 2021|access-date=16 October 2024}}</ref>

The fan-made song "[[Super Max!]]", performed by the {{Ill|Pitstop Boys|nl}} in 2016, went viral after his victory at the [[2021 Abu Dhabi Grand Prix]], topping the [[Spotify]] ''Viral 50'' chart in the Netherlands and reaching number two in the United Kingdom.<ref name="Gamble 2021">{{cite web |last=Gamble |first=Andrew |title=Verstappen's song 'Super Max' goes viral & enters Spotify's top charts |website=Daily Express |date=15 December 2021 |url=https://www.express.co.uk/sport/f1-autosport/1536642/Max-Verstappen-F1-song-Super-Max-goes-viral-Spotify-top-charts |access-date=12 September 2022 |archive-date=26 December 2021 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20211226014407/https://www.express.co.uk/sport/f1-autosport/1536642/Max-Verstappen-F1-song-Super-Max-goes-viral-Spotify-top-charts |url-status=live}}</ref> Another fan-made [[Electronic dance music|EDM]] track "33 Max Verstappen", was released in 2023, eventually going viral on social media due to [[internet meme]]s referencing the hook: "tu tu tu du, Max Verstappen".<ref>{{cite news |title=The New Max Verstappen Anthem An Absolute Banger Taking Over All-Time Favorite Super Max |url=https://www.essentiallysports.com/f1-news-the-new-max-verstappen-anthem-an-absolute-banger-taking-over-all-time-favorite-super-max/ |last=Suhalka |first=Mahim |date=6 September 2022 |work=EssentiallySports |access-date=2 April 2024 |archive-date=2 April 2024 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20240402122316/https://www.essentiallysports.com/f1-news-the-new-max-verstappen-anthem-an-absolute-banger-taking-over-all-time-favorite-super-max/ |url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{cite news |title=On His Birthday, Max Verstappen's Big Spotify Hit Goes Missing |url=https://thesportsrush.com/f1-news-on-his-birthday-max-verstappens-big-spotify-hit-goes-missing/ |last=Gopal Srivastava |first=Naman |date=30 September 2023 |access-date=2 April 2024 |work=The Sports Rush |archive-date=2 April 2024 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20240402122320/https://thesportsrush.com/f1-news-on-his-birthday-max-verstappens-big-spotify-hit-goes-missing/ |url-status=live}}</ref>

== Other racing ==
===Sportscar racing===
[[File:MSR GTP WGI23 11.jpg|thumb|left|The [[Acura ARX-06]] (pictured in 2023) tested by Verstappen with [[Honda Racing Corporation]] in November 2024.]]
Verstappen tested the [[Honda NSX (second generation)#Super GT|Honda NSX-GT]]—a [[Super GT#GT500|GT500]] class [[Super GT]] car—during the 2022 [[Honda Racing Corporation|Honda Racing]] ''Thanks Day'' event at [[Mobility Resort Motegi|Motegi]].<ref>{{Cite web |last=Klein |first=Jamie |date=26 November 2022 |title=Verstappen samples Honda NSX-GT Super GT car |url=https://www.motorsport.com/supergt/news/verstappen-honda-nsx-motegi-test/10405164/ |access-date=30 November 2024 |website=[[Motorsport.com]]}}</ref> After winning the event's [[kart racing|kart race]] alongside [[Marc Márquez]], Honda awarded Verstappen—the first [[Honda in Formula One|Honda-powered]] World Drivers' Champion since {{F1|1991}}—with an [[Honda NSX (second generation)#NSX Type S (2022)|NSX Type S]].<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://news.verstappen.com/en/article/4987 |title=Max full of gratitude at Honda Racing Thanks Day: 'An incredible feeling'|website=Verstappen.com |date=28 November 2022 |access-date=30 November 2024 |first=Stefan |last=Meens}}</ref>

In January 2024, Verstappen drove the [[Ferrari 296 GT3]] at the [[Algarve International Circuit|Portimão]], as part of a two-day test with [[Deutsche Tourenwagen Masters]] team [[Emil Frey Racing]] alongside [[Thierry Vermeulen]],<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.autosport.com/dtm/news/why-verstappen-tested-a-ferrari-gt3-car-for-dtm-team-emil-frey/10563140/ |title=Why Verstappen tested a Ferrari GT3 car for DTM team Emil Frey |date=6 January 2024 |access-date=20 November 2024 |first=Sven |last=Haidinger |website=[[Autosport]]}}</ref> who competed under the ''Verstappen.com Racing'' banner in [[2023 Deutsche Tourenwagen Masters|2023]].<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://news.verstappen.com/en/article/5019|title=Thierry Vermeulen takes the next step to contest DTM |date=24 February 2023 |access-date=20 November 2024 |first=Niels |last=Hendrix |website=Verstappen.com}}</ref> Team principal Lorenz Frey-Hilti stated "there were also other [[Group GT3|GT3]] cars that day, but I can only say that Verstappen was driving on a different level than the rest. There are good drivers who cannot shine in a GT3 car due to a lack of experience, but Max is different. If something has four wheels, then [he's fast in it]", adding that he was "world-class".<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://racingnews365.com/verstappen-amazes-dtm-teamboss-during-test-max-is-on-another-level |title=Verstappen amazes DTM team boss during test: 'Max is on another level' |date=8 January 2024 |access-date=20 November 2024 |first=Rory |last=Mitchell |website=RacingNews365}}</ref>

On 19 November 2024, Verstappen drove a [[Sports prototype|prototype]] for the first time, testing the [[Acura ARX-06]] with [[Honda Racing Corporation]] at [[Las Vegas Motor Speedway]], ahead of the {{F1GP|2024|Las Vegas}}.<ref>{{Cite magazine |url=https://www.roadandtrack.com/news/a62953060/max-verstappen-will-drive-acuras-imsa-gtp-racer-today/ |title=Max Verstappen Will Drive Acura's IMSA GTP Racer Today |date=19 November 2024 |access-date=20 November 2024 |magazine=[[Road & Track]] |first=Fred |last=Smith}}</ref> Verstappen expressed his desire to compete in the [[24 Hours of Daytona]] and [[24 Hours of Le Mans|Le Mans]] after the test, stating that "it's just about finding the time".<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.autosport.com/imsa/news/verstappen-eager-to-race-daytona-24-and-le-mans-after-gtp-car-test/10674741/ |title=Verstappen eager to race Daytona 24 and Le Mans after Acura run |last=Barnes |first=Joey |date=21 November 2024 |website=[[Autosport]] |access-date=8 December 2024}}</ref>

In May 2025, he entered testing for the third round of the [[2025 Nürburgring Langstrecken-Serie]] in the 296 under the [[pseudonym]] "Franz Hermann", where he claimed to set an unofficial [[Group GT3|GT3]] lap record, reportedly in an attempt to obtain a licence to compete on the ''[[Nordschleife]]''.<ref>{{Cite magazine |url=https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/formel-1/max-verstappen-nordschleife-test-ferrari-296-gt3/ |title=Verstappen fährt unter Pseudonym |trans-title=Verstappen drives under a pseudonym |last1=Leppert |first1=Bianca |last2=Grüner |first2=Tobias |date=9 May 2025 |magazine=[[Auto Motor und Sport]] |publisher=[[Gruner + Jahr|Motor Presse Stuttgart]] |language=de |issn=0005-0806 |access-date=9 May 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250509095903/https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/formel-1/max-verstappen-nordschleife-test-ferrari-296-gt3/ |archive-date=9 May 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.espn.com/f1/story/_/id/45163768/incognitio-max-verstappen-explains-breaking-nurburgring-record-alias-franz-hermann |title=Incognitio Max Verstappen explains breaking Nurburgring record under Franz Hermann alias |last=Edmondson |first=Laurence |date=15 May 2025 |website=[[ESPN]] |publisher=[[ESPN Inc.]] |access-date=16 May 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250516103243/https://www.espn.com/f1/story/_/id/45163768/incognitio-max-verstappen-explains-breaking-nurburgring-record-alias-franz-hermann |archive-date=16 May 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref> He received an [[FIA Platinum]] licence later that month, allowing him to compete in top-level [[endurance racing (motorsport)|endurance racing]].<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/what-can-f1-champion-max-verstappen-do-with-his-new-fia-platinum-race-license/10728272/ |title=Verstappen's new race options: What he can do with his FIA platinum race licence? |last1=Stade |first1=Laurens |last2=Cleeren |first2=Filip |date=31 May 2025 |website=[[Autosport]] |publisher=[[Motorsport Network]] |access-date=25 June 2025 |issn=0269-946X |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250601002720/https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/what-can-f1-champion-max-verstappen-do-with-his-new-fia-platinum-race-license/10728272/ |archive-date=1 June 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref>

In September 2025, he made his competitive debut on the ''Nordschleife'' in the seventh round for [[Lionspeed GP]], completing the laps required for a GT3 permit in a [[Engine power|horsepower]]-restricted [[Porsche 718 Boxster and Cayman (982)#718 Cayman GT4 and 718 Spyder|Porsche 718 Cayman GT4 CS]] as he finished seventh in-class.<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.motorsport.com/f1/news/everything-you-need-to-know-about-max-verstappens-nurburgring-race-debut/10758599/ |title=Everything you need to known about Max Verstappen's Nurburgring race debut |last=Cleeren |first=Filip |date=13 September 2025 |website=[[Motorsport.com]] |publisher=[[Motorsport Network]] |access-date=13 September 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250915165723/https://www.motorsport.com/f1/news/everything-you-need-to-know-about-max-verstappens-nurburgring-race-debut/10758599 |archive-date=15 September 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref> Later the same month, Verstappen made his GT3 race debut and won. He drove for [[Emil Frey Racing]], racing a [[Ferrari 296#Ferrari 296 GT3|Ferrari 296 GT3]] with [[Chris Lulham]] in the [[Nürburgring Langstrecken-Serie|Nürburgring Endurance Series]].<ref>{{cite web |url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/verstappen-gt3-debut-nls-nordschleife/10762117/ |title=Verstappen to make GT3 race debut at Nordschleife in NLS this weekend |website=Autosport |last1=Brederlow |first1=Sönke |last2=Hardy |first2=Ed |date=24 September 2025}}</ref> This start also made Max Verstappen the first defending Formula One World Champion to simultaneously compete in Formula One and another racing series since [[Keke Rosberg]], who made a one-off appearance in the [[1983 World Sportscar Championship]], also at the Nürburgring Nordschleife.{{citation needed|date=September 2025}} After qualifying third, Verstappen, who drove the first stint, passed two cars and began to create a gap of over a minute. Verstappen handed the car to teammate Lulham for the second stint; Lulham crossed the line to win the race for the team, 24 seconds ahead of the car in second place.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/verstappen-wins-on-nls-gt3-debut-at-the-nordschleife/10763002/ |title=Verstappen wins on NLS GT3 debut at the Nordschleife |last=Vinel |first=Ben |date=27 September 2025 |website=Autosport}}</ref>

===Sim racing===
In his free time, Verstappen competes in [[sim racing]]. Verstappen stated that it helps him with his real-life racing, keeping him "ready to go".<ref name="RacingNews365">{{cite web |title=How Verstappen uses sim racing to become a better driver |website=RacingNews365 |date=11 January 2022 |url=https://racingnews365.com/how-verstappen-uses-sim-racing-to-become-a-better-driver |access-date=16 January 2022 |archive-date=16 January 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220116135001/https://racingnews365.com/how-verstappen-uses-sim-racing-to-become-a-better-driver |url-status=live}}</ref> Since 2015, Verstappen has been a member of ''Team Redline'', a British-Luxembourgish multinational professional sim racing team.<ref>{{cite web|title=Team Redline › Max Verstappen|url=https://www.teamredline.com/work/max-verstappen/|access-date=22 January 2022|website=Team Redline|archive-date=22 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220122123254/https://www.teamredline.com/work/max-verstappen/|url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.iracing.com/max-verstappen-joins-team-redline/|title=Max Verstappen Joins Team Redline|date=30 July 2015|access-date=24 November 2024|first=Jaime|last=Baker|website=[[iRacing]]}}</ref>

====iRacing Special Events (2020–present)====
{{see also|iRacing}}
Verstappen has competed in several marquee events on ''[[iRacing]]'' since February 2019, when he entered the ''[[Mount Panorama|Bathurst]] 12 Special Event''—a 12-hour virtual team [[endurance racing (motorsport)|endurance race]]—alongside [[Lando Norris]].<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.autosport.com/esports/news/f1-duo-verstappen-and-norris-team-up-for-iracings-bathurst-12-hour-5283532/5283532/|title=F1 duo Verstappen and Norris team-up for iRacing's Bathurst 12 Hour|date=8 February 2019|access-date=26 November 2024|first=Andrew|last=van Leeuwen|website=[[Autosport]]}}</ref> The pair won the ''[[Spa-Francorchamps|Spa]] 24 Special Event'' in July.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.motorsportmagazine.com/articles/single-seaters/f1/verstappen-and-norris-win-spa-24-hour-esports-race/|title=Verstappen and Norris win Spa 24-hour esports race|date=22 July 2019|access-date=26 November 2024|website=[[Motor Sport (magazine)|Motor Sport]]|first=Jake|last=Williams-Smith}}</ref> Verstappen won the ''[[Petit Le Mans]] Special Event'' in October 2020.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.the-race.com/gaming/verstappen-wallmeier-win-iracings-petit-le-mans-event/|title=Verstappen/Wallmeier win iRacing's Petit Le Mans event|date=4 October 2020|access-date=26 November 2024|first=Matt|last=Beer|website=The Race}}</ref> Later that year, [[Haas F1 Team|Haas]] driver and ''R8G eSports'' founder [[Romain Grosjean]] deemed Verstappen to be the "fastest" driver in sim racing.<ref>{{cite web|last=GPfans.com|title=Grosjean zou Verstappen wel in zijn sim-team willen: "Hij is het snelst"|url=https://www.gpfans.com/nl/f1-nieuws/60667/grosjean-zou-verstappen-wel-in-zijn-sim-team-willen-hij-is-het-snelst/|access-date=13 January 2022|website=GPfans|date=21 December 2020 |language=nl|archive-date=13 January 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220113111909/https://www.gpfans.com/nl/f1-nieuws/60667/grosjean-zou-verstappen-wel-in-zijn-sim-team-willen-hij-is-het-snelst/|url-status=live}}</ref> He entered the eighth round of the 2023 ''AMX Global League'' at [[Imola Circuit|Imola]], winning the first heat.<ref name="ATK"/>

Verstappen entered the ''[[Daytona International Speedway|Daytona]] 24 Special Event'' in January 2024, winning the [[IMSA SportsCar Championship#GT Daytona (GTD)|GTD]] class with Redline.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.goodwood.com/grr/race/modern/esports-news--max-verstappen-wins-at-iracing-24-hours-of-daytona/|title=Esports News {{!}} Max Verstappen wins at iRacing 24 Hours of Daytona|date=23 January 2024|access-date=24 November 2024|first=Andrew|last=Evans|website=[[Goodwood Circuit|Goodwood Road & Racing]]}}</ref> In May, Verstappen received media attention for entering the ''[[Nürburgring]] 24 Special Event'' on the same weekend as the {{F1GP|2024|Emilia Romagna}};<ref>{{cite magazine|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/racing/f1briefings/news/f1-news-max-verstappen-wins-24-hour-race-alongside-grand-prix-on-insane-weekend-01hy8mcjz6jb|title=F1 News: Max Verstappen Wins 24-Hour Race Alongside Grand Prix on Insane Weekend|date=19 May 2024|access-date=20 November 2024|first=Alex|last=Harrington|magazine=[[Sports Illustrated]]}}</ref> he won both events, achieving a [[List of Formula One driver records#Most consecutive pole positions|record-equalling]] eighth consecutive pole position at the latter. Following his victory at the former, Ed Hardy of ''[[Autosport]]'' opined that Verstappen had "firmly established himself as one of the best sim racers in the world".<ref name="ATK" /> Verstappen won his second ''Spa 24 Special Event'' in July that year, completing a perfect season of 24-hour special events.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://traxion.gg/max-verstappens-team-redline-win-24-hour-sim-racing-event-on-f1-weekend/|title=Max Verstappen's Team Redline win 24-hour sim racing event on F1 weekend|date=21 July 2024|access-date=24 November 2024|first=Thomas|last=Harrison-Lord|website=Traxion.GG}}</ref>

====Le Mans Virtual (2020–2023)====
{{see also|24 Hours of Le Mans Virtual|Le Mans Virtual Series|rFactor 2}}
Verstappen competed in the [[24 Hours of Le Mans Virtual]] on ''[[rFactor 2]]'' in [[2020 24 Hours of Le Mans Virtual|2020]], [[2022 24 Hours of Le Mans Virtual|2022]] and [[2023 24 Hours of Le Mans Virtual|2023]]—hosted by [[Automobile Club de l'Ouest]] and the [[FIA World Endurance Championship]]—as well as two further rounds of the [[2022–23 Le Mans Virtual Series]] in the [[LMP2]] class.<ref name="ATK">{{cite web|url=https://www.autosport.com/le-mans/news/all-to-know-about-max-verstappens-sim-racing-career-amid-le-mans-24-hours-ambition/10613870/|title=All to know about Max Verstappen's sim racing career amid Le Mans 24 Hours ambition|website=[[Autosport]]|date=22 May 2024|access-date=26 November 2024|first=Ed|last=Hardy}}</ref> He described the former competition as a "clown show" after a technical issue saw him retire from the lead of the 2023 edition,<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.motorsportmagazine.com/articles/e-sports/esports/frustrated-verstappen-brands-virtual-le-mans-a-clown-show-was-it-justified/|title=Frustrated Verstappen brands Virtual Le Mans a 'clown show': was it justified?|date=16 January 2023|access-date=26 November 2024|first=Cambridge|last=Kisby}}</ref> subsequently finishing runner-up in the overall championship to [[Porsche in motorsport|Porsche Coanda]].<ref name="ATK"/>

== Verstappen.com Racing ==
[[File:DTM 2024 Spielberg Nr. 69 Vermeulen.jpg|thumb|right|In 2022, Verstappen founded the [[eponymous]] racing team ''Verstappen.com Racing''.|alt=Thierry Vermeulen driving the Verstappen.com Racing Ferrari 296 GT3 in the 2024 Deutsche Tourenwagen Masters at the Red Bull Ring]]
Verstappen founded the [[eponymous]] racing team ''Verstappen.com Racing'' in 2022, supporting his father Jos in [[rallying]] and [[Thierry Vermeulen]] in the [[ADAC GT Masters]].<ref>{{cite web|url=https://news.verstappen.com/en/article/5723|title=Verstappen.com Racing announces 2025 program. Max: 'Exciting year ahead!'|website=verstappen.com|date=6 March 2025|access-date=13 June 2025|last=Hendrix|first=Niels}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.the-race.com/formula-1/max-verstappen-racing-team-gt3-world-challenge-dtm-2025/ |title=What you should know about Max Verstappen's own team's 2025 plans |last1=Villemant |first1=Thibaut |last2=Khorounzhiy |first2=Valentin |date=6 March 2025 |website=The Race |publisher=The Race Media |access-date=9 March 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250306203816/https://www.the-race.com/formula-1/max-verstappen-racing-team-gt3-world-challenge-dtm-2025/ |archive-date=6 March 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref> The following year, the project expanded to [[2023 GT World Challenge Europe Sprint Cup|GT World Challenge Europe Sprint Cup]] and the [[2023 Deutsche Tourenwagen Masters|Deutsche Tourenwagen Masters]], linking up with [[Ferrari]] customer [[Emil Frey Racing]].<ref>{{Cite magazine |url=https://www.motorsportmagazine.com/articles/single-seaters/f1/can-max-verstappens-new-racing-team-succeed-where-other-f1-champs-failed/ |url-access=registration |title=Can Max Verstappen's new racing team succeed where other F1 champs failed? |last=Elson |first=James |date=6 March 2025 |magazine=[[Motor Sport (magazine)|Motor Sport]] |access-date=9 March 2025 |issn=0027-2019 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250306223009/https://www.motorsportmagazine.com/articles/single-seaters/f1/can-max-verstappens-new-racing-team-succeed-where-other-f1-champs-failed/ |archive-date=6 March 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref> In 2025, in conjunction with this effort, Verstappen.com made its debut as a standalone team in the [[2025 GT World Challenge Europe Endurance Cup|Endurance Cup]], fielding a 2 Seas Motorsport-run [[Aston Martin Vantage (2018)#GT3|Aston Martin Vantage AMR GT3 Evo]] for Vermeulen, [[Harry King (racing driver)|Harry King]] and [[sim racing]] convert [[Chris Lulham]];<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.motorsport.com/gtwce-endurance/news/verstappen-expands-own-team-into-gt-world-challenge-europe/10700804/ |title=Verstappen expands his team into GT World Challenge Europe |last=Watkins |first=Gary |date=6 March 2025 |website=[[Motorsport.com]] |publisher=[[Motorsport Network]] |access-date=4 March 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250306152321/https://www.motorsport.com/gtwce-endurance/news/verstappen-expands-own-team-into-gt-world-challenge-europe/10700804/ |archive-date=6 March 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref> they took the [[GT World Challenge Europe|Gold Cup]] victory at the [[2025 24 Hours of Spa|24 Hours of Spa]].<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/verstappen-watched-spa-24-hours-and-not-f1-austrian-gp-after-race-retirement/10737754/ |title=Verstappen watched Spa 24 Hours and not F1 Austrian GP after race retirement |last=Selleck |first=Emily |date=29 June 2025 |website=[[Autosport]] |publisher=[[Motorsport Network]] |access-date=8 July 2025 |issn=0269-946X |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250701003050/https://www.autosport.com/f1/news/verstappen-watched-spa-24-hours-and-not-f1-austrian-gp-after-race-retirement/10737754/ |archive-date=1 July 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref>

==Personal life==
[[File:PH-DTF Dassault Falcon 900EX (Exxaero).jpg|thumb|left|Verstappen's former aircraft (PH-DTF) at [[Luton Airport]] in 2022]]
Verstappen holds [[dual citizenship|dual]] Dutch and Belgian citizenship, and decided to compete with a Dutch racing licence, as his father [[Jos Verstappen|Jos]] did. He stated in 2014 that he "feels more Dutch", having spent more time with his father than with his mother during his childhood, as well as his upbringing in [[Maaseik]], a Belgian town at the Dutch border.<ref name="nationality2" /> The following year, Verstappen added that he "actually only lived in Belgium to sleep", and that he "was raised as a Dutch person and that's how [he feels]".<ref>{{Cite web|url=https://www.gpfans.com/nl/artikelen/41751/dit-is-waarom-max-verstappen-niet-onder-de-belgische-vlag-rijdt/|title=Dit is waarom Max Verstappen niet onder de Belgische vlag rijdt|website=GPfans|date=3 July 2019 |access-date=1 November 2020|archive-date=19 September 2020|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200919153745/https://www.gpfans.com/nl/artikelen/41751/dit-is-waarom-max-verstappen-niet-onder-de-belgische-vlag-rijdt/|url-status=live}}</ref> In 2022, Verstappen stated that he appreciates both sides and is "half-half at the end of the day".<ref>{{Cite web |last=Moeke |first=Patrick |date=1 September 2022 |title='Belg' Verstappen vroeg direct toen hij achttien jaar werd Nederlands paspoort aan. |trans-title='Belgian' Verstappen applied for Dutch passport right after turning eighteen. |url=https://www.nu.nl/formule-1/6221291/belg-verstappen-vroeg-direct-toen-hij-achttien-jaar-werd-nederlands-paspoort-aan.html |website=NU |publisher=[[MPG Media]] |language=NL-nl}}</ref> In addition to his [[Dutch language|native]] Dutch, he is fluent in English and [[German language|German]], with limited proficiency in [[French language|French]].<ref>{{Cite magazine |url=https://www.beano.com/facts/sport/max-verstappen-facts |url-access=registration |title=Make Vroom in Your Brain for These 15 Max Verstappen Facts! |magazine=[[The Beano]] |publisher=[[Beano Studios]] |access-date=21 July 2025 |oclc=28686914 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250517060336/https://www.beano.com/facts/sport/max-verstappen-facts |archive-date=17 May 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref>

He competed in Formula One for over a season before obtaining a road driving licence on his 18th birthday.<ref>{{cite web|title=Road legal at last! Verstappen passes driving test on 18th birthday|url=http://www.formula1.com/content/fom-website/en/latest/headlines/2015/9/verstappen-passes-driving-test-on-18th-birthday.html|publisher=Formula One|access-date=30 September 2015|date=30 September 2015|archive-date=31 July 2016|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160731183946/http://www.formula1.com/content/fom-website/en/latest/headlines/2015/9/verstappen-passes-driving-test-on-18th-birthday.html|url-status=live}}</ref> Verstappen moved to [[Monaco]] in October 2015 where he has lived ever since.<ref name="GPNov15">{{cite news|url=http://www.grandprix.com/ns/ns32591.html|title=Verstappen moves out of home for Monaco|date=28 November 2015|work=GrandPrix.com|access-date=17 May 2016|archive-date=22 May 2016|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160522214846/http://www.grandprix.com/ns/ns32591.html|url-status=live}}</ref> In November 2020, Verstappen bought a [[Dassault Falcon 900|Dassault Falcon 900EX]] aircraft from [[Virgin Galactic]]. The aircraft, [[aircraft registration|registered]] PH-DTF, was operated by Exxaero.<ref name=Falcon>{{cite web |url=https://www.aviation24.be/tour-operators/exxaero/formula-one-racing-driver-max-verstappen-acquires-former-virgin-galactic-private-jet/ |title=Formula One racing driver Max Verstappen acquires former Virgin Galactic private jet |first=Bart |last=Noeth |publisher=Aviation24 |date=20 November 2021 |access-date=26 January 2022 |archive-date=26 January 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220126173346/https://www.aviation24.be/tour-operators/exxaero/formula-one-racing-driver-max-verstappen-acquires-former-virgin-galactic-private-jet/ |url-status=live}}</ref> In January 2025, he acquired a Mangusta GranSport 33 yacht, named ''Unleash the Lion''.<ref name=Yacht>{{cite web |url=https://www.planetf1.com/news/first-look-max-verstappen-takes-delivery-of-15m-superyacht/ |title=First look: Max Verstappen takes delivery of his huge $15m superyacht |first=Michelle |last=Foster |publisher=Planetf1 |date=22 January 2025 |access-date=16 February 2025 |archive-date=22 January 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250122121645/https://www.planetf1.com/news/first-look-max-verstappen-takes-delivery-of-15m-superyacht/ |url-status=live}}</ref> The following month, he sold the Falcon 900EX and purchased a [[Dassault Falcon 8X]], registered PH-UTL, which is also operated by Exxaero.<ref name=Falcon8x>{{cite web |url=https://www.si.com/fannation/racing/f1briefings/news/max-verstappen-buys-58-million-private-jet-this-is-what-we-know-01jm3gg4tqrb |title=Max Verstappen Buys $58 Million Private Jet – This Is What We Know |first=Alex |last=Harrington |publisher=Sports Illustrated |date=14 February 2025 |access-date=16 February 2025}}</ref>

Verstappen is a supporter of [[FC Barcelona]] and [[PSV Eindhoven]].<ref>{{Cite web |last=Moerman |first=Christian |date=26 November 2021 |title=Verstappen over favoriete club FC Barcelona: 'Ik baalde van vertrek Koeman' |trans-title=Verstappen about favorite club FC Barcelona: "I was disappointed with the departure of Koeman" |url=https://www.f1maximaal.nl/max-verstappen/verstappen-over-favoriete-club-fc-barcelona-ik-baalde-van-vertrek-koeman |access-date=16 May 2022 |website=F1Maximaal.nl |language=nl |archive-date=31 May 2022 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220531140907/https://www.f1maximaal.nl/max-verstappen/verstappen-over-favoriete-club-fc-barcelona-ik-baalde-van-vertrek-koeman |url-status=live}}</ref> 

In September 2022, he was appointed an Officer of the [[Order of Orange-Nassau]] in recognition of his racing achievements.<ref name="auto">{{Cite web|url=https://nltimes.nl/2022/09/01/royal-honor-max-verstappen-appointed-officer-order-oranje-nassau|title=Royal honor for Max Verstappen: Appointed Officer in the Order of Oranje-Nassau|access-date=1 September 2022|date=1 September 2022|website=NL Times|archive-date=13 October 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20221013203401/https://nltimes.nl/2022/09/01/royal-honor-max-verstappen-appointed-officer-order-oranje-nassau|url-status=live}}</ref>

Verstappen has been dating Brazilian-Dutch model [[Kelly Piquet]], daughter of three-time World Drivers' Champion [[Nelson Piquet]], since October 2020.<ref>{{cite web |author1=Bethânia Nunes |author2=Marina Ferreira |title=Modelo Kelly Piquet assume romance com piloto da Fórmula 1 Max Verstappen |url=https://www.metropoles.com/colunas/claudia-meireles/modelo-kelly-piquet-assume-romance-com-piloto-da-formula-1-max-verstappen |website=Metrópoles |access-date=13 March 2021 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210313152034/https://www.metropoles.com/colunas/claudia-meireles/modelo-kelly-piquet-assume-romance-com-piloto-da-formula-1-max-verstappen |archive-date=13 March 2021 |language=pt |date=1 January 2021 |url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.usmagazine.com/celebrity-news/pictures/max-verstappen-and-kelly-piquets-relationship-timeline/|website=Us Weekly|date=15 October 2024|access-date=2 November 2024|title=Max Verstappen and Kelly Piquet's Relationship Timeline|archive-date=15 May 2023|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230515125609/https://www.usmagazine.com/celebrity-news/pictures/max-verstappen-and-kelly-piquets-relationship-timeline/|url-status=live}}</ref> They had their first daughter in April 2025.<ref>{{Cite web |url=https://www.motorsport.com/f1/news/max-verstappen-speaks-out-for-the-first-time-since-becoming-a-dad/10718883/ |title=Max Verstappen speaks out for the first time since becoming a dad |last=Selleck |first=Emily |date=2 May 2025 |website=[[Motorsport.com]] |publisher=[[Motorsport Network]] |access-date=2 May 2025 |quote=Max Verstappen revealed he "got to spend a few days" with partner Kelly Piquet and their new daughter before flying to the Miami Grand Prix |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250502232505/https://www.motorsport.com/f1/news/max-verstappen-speaks-out-for-the-first-time-since-becoming-a-dad/10718883/ |archive-date=2 May 2025 |url-status=live}}</ref>
